‘eṇṇaṉpa ēṉai eḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum

kaṇṇaṉpa vāḻum uyirkku (kuṟaḷ—392)





eṇ eḻuttu ikaḻēl (ātticūṭi—7)





eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇeṉat takum



(koṉṟai vēntaṉ-7)





‘kaṟkak kacaṭaṟak kaṟpavai kaṟṟapiṉ

kiṟka ataṟkut takaveṉṟa-coṟkuḷḷē

ellārkkum putti iyampik karaiyēṟṟa

vallāy uṉakku uraikka vallēṉō’



(tamiḻviṭu tātu—97, 98)

kaṭanta aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷil 1930il kumārarājā (rājā) muttaiyac ceṭṭiyār (nītikkaṭci) kalvip poṟuppil irunta nāḷ toṭṭu, iṉṟu (1991) māṇpumiku irāma. vīrappaṉ avarkaḷ attuṟaiyiṉai vakikkum nāḷ varaiyil tamiḻakak kalvit tuṟaiyiṉai — vaḷarcciyiṉai — māṟṟattiṉaik kaṇṭavaṉātaliṉ — aṉṟu mutal iṉṟu varai ataṉoṭu toṭarpu uṭaiyavaṉ ātaliṉ innūliṉai eḻuta muṟpaṭṭēṉ.

a.mu.pa.





muṉṉurai

kaṟka kacaṭaṟak kaṟpavai kaṟṟapiṉ

niṟka ataṟkut taka' eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar coṉṉa kuṟaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyilēyē inta nūl eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu. itaṟku viḷakkam tanta parimēlaḻakar ‘itaṉāl kaṟkappaṭum nūlkaḷum kaṟkumāṟum kaṟṟataṉāl payaṉum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa’ eṉkiṟār. inta muṟaiyil iṉṟaiya nāṭṭuk kalvi irukkiṟatā eṉpatai nallōr eṇṇip pārkkavum illaiyāṉāl vaḷḷuvar piṟanta nāṭṭil avar moḻinta vakaiyil kalvittuṟaiyai amaikkavum vēṇṭiyē innūl eḻutappeṟṟatu.

nāṭṭil nām kaṟkiṟōm. āṉāl kaṟpavaṟṟai-kaṟka vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaik kaṟkiṟōmā? illaiyē! iṉṟaiya kalvi makkaḷ vāḻkkaikku-camutāya vaḷarccikku ēṟṟatāka uḷḷatā? ām eṉṟu yārāvatu kūṟamuṭiyumā ?

cari, avvāṟu kaṟpaṉavaṟṟaik kacaṭuaṟa-piḻaiyaṟā (pariti) mācuaṟa (kāḷiṅkar) kuṟṟamaṟak (maṇakkuṭavar) kaṟkiṟōmā? atuvum illaiyē. ārampappaḷḷi mutal kallūri mutukalai varaiyil avvāṟu māṇavarkaḷ kaṟṟāl-kaṟkum vakaiyil āciriyarkaḷ āṟṟuppaṭuttiṉāl tērvukaḷil nūṟṟukku nūṟu tēṟa vēṇṭāmā? āṉāl anta nilai illaiyē!

aṭuttu, kaṟṟapaṭiyāvatu niṟkiṟōmā? atuvum illaiyē! ‘tām iṉpuṟuvatu ulaku iṉpuṟak kaṇṭu kāmuṟuvar kaṟṟaṟintār’ eṉṟa kuṟaḷukku ēṟpa, aṉaivarum kaṟṟapaṭi naṭantāl nāṭṭil ‘ellārum ellāc celvamum’. peṟṟu iṉpamāka vāḻum nāṭākattāṉē itu irukkum. āṉāl anta iṉpam eṅkē irukkiṟatu?

eṉavē nāṭṭil tēvaiyāṉa-camutāya vāḻvai vaḷam paṭuttum kalvi illai eṉpatu kaṇ kūṭu. itai oruvāṟu viḷakkum vakaiyilē ovvoru pakutiyum innūlil eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷatu. akkuṟaikaḷai nīkka vaḻikaḷum ōraḷavu cuṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

tirukkuṟaḷil poruṭpālil araciyal, toṭakkattē mutal atikāramāṉa iṟaimāṭcikku (38) aṭutta atikāramākavē kalvi (89) amaikiṉṟatu etaik kāṭṭukiṟatu? kalviyai - tēvaiyāṉa kalviyait tam makkaḷukku aḷittalē araciṉ mutaṟ kaṭamai eṉpatait tāṉē itu viḷakkukiṟatu. nāṭṭilē kalvi nalam peṟavillaiyāṉāl, vēṟu etuvum nallamuṟaiyil amaiyātu eṉa aṉṟutoṭṭu iṉṟu varai ellārum kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. aṉṟu aracukku aṟamuraitta vaḷḷuvar toṭaṅki iṉṟu nam nāṭṭu-urimai nāṭṭup peruntalaivar paṇṭita nēru uṭpaṭa aṉaivarum ikkaruttai valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉarē. 1961il iṉṟaikku muppatu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ intiya orumaippāṭṭuk kuḻu amaikka ēṟpāṭu ceyta mānāṭṭil paṇṭita nēru avarkaḷ, nāṭṭu orumaippāṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭu vāḻa-nāṉilam vāḻa-camutāyam cīrpeṟak kalviyē mukkiyam eṉpataṉai valiyuṟuttik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. anta mānāṭṭil ellā araciyal kaṭcit talaivarkaḷum mattiya amaiccarkaḷum mānila mutal amaiccarkaḷum, palkalaik kaḻakat tuṇaivēntarkaḷum kalvi vallunarkaḷum aṟiviyal mētaikaḷum, toḻil mutalvarkaḷum kūṭi iruntaṉarē. (1961-cep 28 mutal ak 1 varai) anta mānāṭṭil kūṭi irunta attaṉai pērum iṉṟuvaraiyil kalviyil nalampeṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷ eṉṉa kaṇṭārkaḷ? eṇṇippārkka innūl cuṭar viḷakkāyiṉum naṉṟāy viḷakkiṭum tūṇṭukōlāka amaikiṉṟatu. paṇṭitar nēru avarkaḷ kūṟiyatil oru pakutiyai iṅkē tarukiṟēṉ.

“Education provides the most important means of bringing about National integration. Since the Problems of National Integration or of national unity essentially, involue the attitudés of groups or large sections of the community and since Education in its brodest sense has been recognised as a powerful instrument for influencing or modifying these attitudes, the conference regards the process of Education and its re-orientation where necessary, as of primary importance” (The Hindu. 17. 11.91)

intak karuttiṉai avar vaḻiyē nāṭṭai āḷum mattiya aracō mānila aracukaḷō eṇṇip pārttu uṭaṉ tiruttiyirukka vēṇṭumē. iṉṟu, aṉṟu illāta vakaiyil ettaṉaiyō vēṟupāṭukaḷ muḷaittut talai virittāṭukiṉṟaṉavē. ippōtum ittakaiya pallōr kalanta nalam kāṇum kūṭṭaṅkaḷai iṉṟaiya piratamarum naṭattukiṟār. ulaka vaṅki pōṉṟa paṉṉāṭṭu niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ nam nāṭṭil kalvi-peṇ kalvi mika iḻi nilaiyil uḷḷataic cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. (The Hindu- 22.11.91) eṉavē talaivarkaḷ-aṟiñarkaḷ, kalvit tuṟaiyiṉaik kaṭṭi āḷupavarkaḷ paṇṭita nēru kūṟiyataiyum ulakattār paḻippataiyum eṇṇi uṭaṉ ceyalāṟṟa innūl oru tūṇṭukōlāka amaiya vēṇṭum eṉptē eṉ ācai.-- vēṇṭukōḷ.

ellāvaṟṟiṉum nallaṉavum uṇṭu; allaṉavum uṇṭu. eṉavē aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu anniyar āṭciyil irunta kalvi muṟaiyiṉaip paṟṟik kūṟi, atil uḷḷa nallaṉavaṟṟai eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉavum allaṉavaṟṟai viṭṭu vēṟu tiruttiya neṟikku māṟa vēṇṭumeṉavum nūlil aṅkaṅkē cuṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. pala nallaṉa māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai eṇṇip pārkkavum vēṇṭiyuḷḷēṉ.

cutantiram peṟṟapiṉ mānila, mattiya aracukaḷ ikkalviyiṉ cīrtiruttam paṟṟiyum māṟṟam paṟṟiyum ettaṉaiyō kuḻukkaḷai amaittaṉa. avaiyum palap pala vakaikaḷ āyntu palvēṟu karuttukkaḷai ēṭṭil vaṭittut tantaṉa. āyiṉum avai ‘kūṭṭattil kūṭi niṉṟu kūṭip pitaṟṟal iṉṟi, nāṭṭattil koḷḷāraṭi-kiḷiyē nāḷil maṟappāraṭi’ eṉṟa pāratiyār vākkiṉ vaḻitāṉē niṟkiṉṟaṉa. aṇmaiyil tiru. irāmamūrtti avarkaḷ talaimaiyil intiya aracu amaitta kalvik kuḻuvum, nāṭṭu nalam kāṇattakka kalvi muṟaiyiṉai ōraḷavu varaiyaṟuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu. ataṉ karuttukkaḷ. cilavaṟṟaiyum appaṭiyē innūlil , (āṅkilattil) cērttuḷḷēṉ.

atil, ōriṭattil kalviyil peṟa vēṇṭiya māṟṟaṅkaḷaic cuṭṭi, avvāṟu nalla neṟiyil kalvi tiruttam peṟāviṭṭāl, attakaiya kalviyaik kaṟpataik kāṭṭilum kallātiruppatē mēl eṉavum kuṟittuḷḷār. (If this is what our education has done to us, one may well ask, is not no education better than bad education— Page V). itu eṉakku, ‘kallāta pērkaḷē nallavarkaḷ nallavarkaḷ’ eṉṟa tāyumāṉavar aṭiyai niṉaivūṭṭiṟṟu. tāyumāṉavar ‘kaṟṟum aṟivillāta eṉ kaṉmattai eṉ colluvēṉ’ eṉṟu naintu urukiyataiyum niṉaikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. iṉṟaiya mattiya aracum mānila aracukaḷum inta neṟi vaḻiyaip piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum. pāṭaṅkaḷ cemmaiyākkap peṟavēṇṭum. āciriyar paṭṭattukku ‘nallāciriyar’kaḷāka aṉṟi uṇmaiyilēyē nallāciriyarkaḷākat tammiṭam payilvārai vāḻa vaikkavēṇṭum. peṟṟōrkaḷum piṟavaṟṟaik kāṭṭilum tam makkaḷ kaṇṇākiya kalviyil nalam peṟuvaḻiyē utavi ceyyavēṇṭum. māṇavarkaḷum iṉṟaiya cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻalil cikkāmal kaṟpaṉa kaṟṟu, ataṉ vaḻi niṉṟu nāṭṭai vaḷamākka muyalavēṇṭum. intap pērācaiyiṉ kāraṇamākavē nāṉ inta nūlai eḻutiṉēṉ.

inta nūl nāṭṭukkalvi oṉṟiṉaiyē nāṭṭamākak koṇṭu eḻutap peṟṟamaiyiṉ atil taṟpōtu uḷḷa kuṟaikaḷai aṅkaṅkē meṉmaiyākavum vaṉmaiyākavum cuṭṭik kāṭṭiṉēṉ. itaṉāl nāṉ yāraiyum kuṟai kūṟiṉēṉ eṉṟō, kuṟaittu matippiṭṭēṉ eṉṟō yārum eṇṇavēṇṭāmeṉak kēṭṭukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ.

eppaṭiyāyiṉum nammuṭaiya paranta pāratamum ciṟanta tamiḻakamum paṇṭaip perumaikaḷait tirumpap peṟṟu, ulaka nāṭukaḷukkiṭaiyil uyarnta oṉṟāka-piṟa nāṭukaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka āṉmīka neṟi, camutāyaneṟi. muṟaipiṟaḻā aracaneṟi, kalvineṟi ākiya aṉaittum mika uyarnta iṭattiṉaip peṟṟuc ciṟakkavēṇṭum eṉṟa ācai -kuṟikkōḷ aṭippaṭaiyilēyē icciṟunūlai veḷiyiṭukiṉṟēṉ. nāṭṭiluḷḷa nallavar - vallavar-āṭciyāḷarkaḷ-aṟiñarkaḷ nallavaṟṟai ēṟṟu nāṭṭai vaḷañceytu kalviyiṉai nēriya vaḻiyil ompi ataṉ vaḻi nāṭṭu nalaṉaik kāttu, nāṭu uyarntu ōṅka vaḻikāṇa vēṇṭum eṉa vēṇṭi vaṇaṅki amaikiṉṟēṉ.

tamiḻkkalai illam

ceṉṉai-30 1.12.91

paṇivuḷḷa,

a.mu. paramacivāṉantam



* * *



acciṭumpōtu piḻaikaḷai ottu nōkkit tirutti utaviya pērāciriyar tiru. cā. vaḷavaṉ avarkaḷukku eṉ naṉṟi urittu. .

a.mu.pa





﻿1. kalvi eṉum kaṇ



* * *



ulakam cuḻaṉṟu varum vēkattil makkaḷ uṇarvu ūcalāṭukiṉṟatu. ki.pi. 2000il itaic ceyvōm, ataic ceyvōm—nāṭṭai poṉṉāṭākkuvōm—viṇmuṭṭap pukaḻ parappuvōm eṉṟu mēlai nāṭukaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟi. paranta pāratamum ataṉ aṅkamāka uḷḷa tamiḻakamum nāḷtōṟum muḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl ceyal muṟaiyil attakaiya, nāṭṭamum ūkkamum uṇarvum vēkamum kāṭṭavillai:

"kūṭṭattil kūṭiniṉṟu kūṭippitaṟṟal iṉṟi

nāṭṭattil koḷḷāraṭi-kiḷiyē

nāḷil maṟappāraṭi"





eṉṟu pāratiyār inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil kūṟiya kūṟṟu. iṉṟum meymmaiyāka aṉṟō uḷḷatu. ‘colvatu oṉṟu ceyvatu oṉṟu’ eṉpatu iṉṟaiya vaḻakkamākiviṭṭatu. araciyalaic cūtāṭṭam eṉpar. āṉāl aṟiviyal, āṉmaviyal, camūkaviyal, paṇpāṭṭiyal yāvumē cūtāṭṭamākavaṉṟō iṉṟu vaṭiveṭuttu ulavukiṉṟatu. pārati pāṭi oru nūṟṟāṇṭu-avaṉ maṟaintu mukkāl nūṟṟāṇṭu, urimai peṟṟu arai nūṟṟāṇṭu ippaṭi nūṟṟāṇṭu ellai kaḻintum intiya makkaḷ—tamiḻ makkaḷ entavakaiyil muṉṉēṟi uḷḷaṉar eṉṟu colla muṭiyum? eṇṇip pāruṅkaḷ!

‘vañcamiṉṟi vāḻumiṉ’ eṉṟu periyavarkaḷ valiyuṟuttiya nāṭṭil-vaḷḷuvar piṟanta nāṭṭil vāḻkkai neṟi eppaṭi varampiṉṟi vīḻntu, nāṭṭaiyum ulakaiyum nalivuṟuttukiṉṟatu. iṉṟu palar pēcuvatu

pōṉṟu 'āṅkilēyaṉ āṭciyē mēl' eṉṟa uṇarvu tāṉē palarukku uṇṭākiṉṟatu. uṇavilē, uṭaiyilē, ūrp payaṇattilē, uṟṟa ceyalkaḷilē maṉitaṉ māṟi viṭṭālum kavalai illai. kālam avaṉait tirutti viṭum eṉa nampalām. āṉāl uḷḷattāl-uḷḷat tuṇarntu kāṇum paṇpāṭṭāl-appaṇpāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē mukiḻkkum ceyalāl avaṉ māṟiviṭṭāl avaṉai eṉṟum yārālum tirutta muṭiyātē! camutāyattil, taṉṉai oru aṅkamākkikkoṇṭu, tāṉ camutāyattukkāka vāḻavēṇṭiyatu makkaḷ niyati. nām camutāyattukku-uyiriṉattukku iṉṟu eṉṉa ceytōm eṉa avaṉ iravil paṭukkap pōkumpōtu eṇṇip pārkkak kaṭamaippaṭṭavaṉ. āṉāl iṉṟaiya maṉitaṉ, eṉakku maṟṟavar iṉṟu eṉṉa ceytārkaḷ-nāṉ yār yāriṭam kaiyūṭṭu vāṅkiṉēṉ-mottam evvaḷavu āyiṟṟu-ēṉ? ettaṉai pērukkut tīṅkiḻaittēṉettaṉai pēraik kolai ceytēṉ eṉṟallavā kaṇakkiṭṭuk koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ. pōrkkaḷattil kaḷam kāṇavēṇṭiya-ceyalāṟṟa vēṇṭiya paṭaikkalaṉkaḷ, iṉṟu vīṭutoṟum-terutoṟum-cantitoṟum ōyātu olippataiyum pāḻpaṭuttuvataiyum nām kāṇkiṟōm. 'tuppākki kalācāram' (Gun Culture) eṉṟu 'piṟaraic cuṭutalākiya paṇpāṭē' eṉṟu pēcuvataṉṟō iṉṟu nāṭṭil naṭamāṭukiṉṟatu. māṇavaṉ āciriyaraik kaṇṭu payantu-paṇintu vaṇaṅkiya kālam pōy āciriyar māṇavaraik kaṇṭu payantu naṭuṅkum kālam vantuviṭṭatu. nāṭāḷum maṉṉarkaḷ accamiṉṟi iravilum. pakalilum taṉiyāka nāṭṭu nalaṉ kāṇa varumpōtu makkaḷ añciyum aṉpuḷattālum pōṟṟi vaṇaṅkiya nilaimāṟi, āḷukiṉṟavarkaḷ makkaḷukku añci aṭukkaṭukkākak kāval cūḻa aṉṟō veḷi varavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. vīṭutōṟum kalaiyiṉ viḷakkam eṉṟu kēṭṭavarkaḷukkellām putuppaḷḷikaḷ, kallūrikaḷ tiṟakka vāri vaḻaṅkiya paṇpāṭu nīṅki, iṉṟu

paḷḷiyō kallūriyō toṭaṅkavō aṉṟip putupputup pāṭaṅkaḷ peṟavō ilaṭcakkaṇakkākak kaiyūṭṭu taravēṇṭiya kālamākavaṉṟō iṉṟu māṟiviṭṭatu. inta nāṭṭil lañcam tāṇṭavamāṭāta iṭam eṅkum illai eṉṟa perumaikku iṅkuḷḷavarkaḷ vaḻivakuttu uḷḷārkaḷē. ciṟu ūḻiyarkaḷ pattu, nūṟu eṉṟa aḷavil niṟka, periyavarkaḷ-uyarnilaiyāḷarkaḷ, āṭciyāḷarkaḷ ilaṭcakkaṇakkil kōṭikkaṇakkil kaiyūṭṭu vāṅkuvatutāṉē iṉṟaiya vāḻkkai neṟi, ‘campaḷam kimpaḷam’ eṉṟu vēṭikkaikkākac collum coṟṟoṭar iṉṟu vāḻkkaiyil mukkiya iṭam peṟṟu viṭṭatē. paḷḷiyilum kallūriyilum cērppataṟku āyirak kaṇakkilum ilaṭcak kaṇakkilum vārikkoṭṭa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. appaṭiyē vēlai peṟat takuti vēṇṭātu lañcamē aḷavu kōlāka amaiya ilaṭcattiṉ ellaiyiṉaiyum atu kaṭakkiṉṟatē!-inta avala nilaiyil, ‘maṉitaṉ eṅkē celkiṟāṉ?’ eṉṟa kēḷvi eḻukiṉṟatē inta nilaiyil pārati kūṟiyapaṭi ‘nāṭṭattil koḷvatu’ eṅkē? eppaṭi muṭiyum?

pala poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟi eṇṇam eḻukiṟatu. iṅkē mutalil kalviyaip paṟṟik kāṇalām. paḻaṅkālattil kalvi pōṟṟap peṟṟamai ‘kurukula vācam’ eṉṟa vakaiyāl nām aṟikiṟōm. taṉitta kuruviṉiṭm, avarkaḷ vīṭṭup paṇikaḷaiyum ceytu koṇṭu, kuṭumpamākac celvarum vaṟiyarum vēṟupāṭu iṉṟi, kalvi kaṟṟu muṉṉēṟi nāṭṭai vāḻvittaṉar. vaṟiñarāṉa kucēlarum araca maintaṉāṉa kaṇṇaṉum orē kuruviṭam vēṟupāṭiṉṟik kaṟṟaṉar. pala pēraṟiñarkaḷ araca kumārarkaḷaiyum āṇṭikaḷiṉ piḷḷaikaḷaiyum tammuṭaṉ irutti, takka vakaiyil kalvi kaṟpittataip paḻaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl iṉṟu anta nilaiyillai. āciriyarkaḷum naṉṉūlāciriyar kāṭṭiya nallāciriyarkaḷ pōṉṟu kōlṭu smit eṉṟa āṅkilap pulavar kāṭṭiya eṉṟa āṅkilap pulavar kāṭṭiya :kirāmap paḷḷi āciriyar pōṉṟu muṟṟum kaṟṟu uṇarntavarkaḷāy-ūrukku vaḻikāṭṭum uttamarāy, etuvariṉum kalaṅkāta uḷa uram uṭaiyavarāy-taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷarāy-talai nimirntu malaiyiṉum māṇāp paṇpu peṟṟa periyavarāy vāḻntuvantaṉar. āṉāl iṉṟaiya āciriyarkaḷ nilaiyiṉai eṇṇip pārkkak kūṭavillai. eṅkō oru cilar paḻaiya paṇpāṭṭu neṟi māṟāmal vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. eṉṟālum perumpālāṉa āciriyarkaḷ tāḻvuṟṟut taṟikeṭṭuttāṉē vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi māṇavar naṭantāl eṉṉākum? paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟuvatilum ettaṉai ēmāṟṟam. pala palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ kāḷāṉkaḷākat tōṉṟi-aracu tarum urimam iṉṟiyē (aṅkīkāramiṉṟiyē) ceyalpaṭṭu, pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷai īrttu, piṉ naṭutteruvil. niṟkaviṭukiṉṟaṉa. rupāy irunūṟum muṉṉūṟum tantāl ṭākṭar paṭṭam peṟa vāyppum uṇṭē! ivai paṟṟiyellām nāḷtōṟum nāḷitaḻkaḷil ceytikaḷaik kāṇkiṉṟōmē! paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟuvataippaṟṟiyum nakaippukkiṭamāṉa vakaiyil pala ceytikaḷ varukiṉṟaṉavē. ‘ṭākṭar’ paṭṭam peṟuvataṟkup paṇamē mukkiyam. paṭippu mukkiyamillai eṉṟu cila āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ oru ciṟanta nāḷitaḻil aṉṉaiyār oruvar eḻutiya kaṭṭurai eṉ niṉaivukku varukiṉṟatu. atai yārāvatu -entap palkalaik kaḻakamāvatu maṟuttatuṇṭā? uṇmaiyai eppaṭi maṟukkamuṭiyum?

mēlai nāṭukaḷil ciṟappāka amerikkāvil tamiḻ nāṭṭup paḻaṅkālak kalvi muṟaiyiṉai, nāṉ cila āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ceṉṟapōtu: kaṇṭu makiḻntēṉ. eṉ nūlilum (ēḻu nāṭukaḷil, eḻupatu nāṭkaḷ) atupaṟṟik kuṟittuḷḷēṉ. orē pāṭattukku. nāṉku āciriyar eṉṟa avala nilai aṅkillai. orē pērāciriyariṉ kīḻ, orē

teḷinta pāṭattiṉai eṭuttuk koṇṭu, avariṭamē muṟaiyākap payiṉṟu, nūl nilaiyattiṉai naṉku payaṉpaṭutti, takka vakaiyil cila viṭaṅkaḷil āṇṭu ellai kūṭa iṉṟip paṭṭam peṟṟu veḷiyēṟukiṉṟaṉar. avartam teḷinta pulamaiyai ulakam viyantu pōṟṟukiṉṟatu. itu namnāṭṭup paḻakkālak kurukula vākkaiyiṉaiyē niṉaivūṭṭukiṟataṉṟō! aṅkē tamiḻum anta vakaiyilēyē payiṟṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. ‘oḷavaiyār’ pāṭalkaḷ oṉṟaṉaiyē pāṭamākap peṟṟu, orē āciriyariṉ kīḻp paṭṭam peṟap payiṉṟavarai nāṉ kaṇṭēṉ. appaṭiyē ṭākṭar paṭṭam peṟuvatum āḻntu payiṉṟu teḷintālaṉṟi iyalātu. peṉcilvēṉiyā palkalaikkaḻakattē tamiḻnāṭṭup poruḷ paṟṟiya (worship of Sun God in Tamilnadu during nineth century) kaṭṭurai ‘em.e.’ vakuppiṟku āyvuk kaṭṭuraiyāka amaiya, pala tērvāḷarkaḷōṭu nāṉum oruvarāy iruntēṉ. nila nūl, tamiḻ, vāṉanūl pōṉṟa pala nūlkaḷ kaṟṟa pala tuṟaiyiṉar 36 pēr kūṭṭi, antakkaṭṭuraiyai ārāyntu, kēṭṭa kēḷvikḷukkellām. anta māṇavi patil aḷittār. ciṟanta vakaiyil amaintatāl paṭṭamum peṟṟār.

iṅkē ṭākṭar paṭṭat tērviṟku ammāṇavariṉ mēṟpārvaiyāḷarē mukkiya tērvāḷar. avarukku vēṇṭiya vēṟu iruvar uṭaṉ tērvāḷarkaḷ. piṉ etai eḻutiṉum paṭṭam peṟat taṭai eṉṉa?

eṅkaḷ kallūriyil, tamiḻil mutal vakuppil tēṟiya oruvar tamiḻāciriyarāka iruntār. oru nāḷ avarai aḻaittu, ‘vākkuṇṭām’ eṉṟa nūlaip paṟṟi ētēṉum teriyumā eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ. āmām ‘vākkuṇṭām nalla maṉmuṇṭām’ eṉṟa pāṭal irukkiṟatu eṉṟu yōcittuc coṉṉār. āṉāl anta nūlaip paṟṟiyō ataṉ āciriyaraip paṟṟiyō avr eḻutiya vēṟu nūlkaḷaip

paṟṟiyō oṉṟum teriyavillai. nallavēḷai avarkaḷ ippōtu paṇiyil illai.

orumuṟai tērvāṇaiyattil kallūri āciriyarait tērvu ceyyum paṇikku aḻaikkappeṟṟēṉ. aṅkē vanta oruvar ‘em.ē.’ vakuppil caiva ilakkiyaṅkaḷaic ciṟappup pāṭamāka eṭuttiruntār. eṉavē nāṉ avarkaḷai nōkki, mutal mūṉṟu tirumuṟaikaḷaip pāṭiyavar yār? eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ. uṭaṉē tayakkamillāmal ‘tirunāvukkaracar’ eṉṟār. uṟuppiṉar aṉaivarum cirittaṉar. eṉ pakkattilirunta mukamatiya uṟuppiṉar ‘aiyā, eṉakkut terikiṟatē, ivarkaḷukkut teriya villaiyē’ eṉṟu varuntiṉār. anta āṇṭu avarait tērvu ceyyavillai. eṉiṉum aṭutta āṇṭu eppaṭiyō iṭampiṭittu, ippoḻutu oru kallūriyil pērāciriyarāka irukkiṟār. itupōṉṟu ettaṉaiyō cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭa muṭiyum.

tamiḻil maṭṭumiṉṟi piṟamoḻikaḷilum piṟapāṭaṅkaḷilum itē nilaiyiṉaik kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. eṅkaḷ kallūri, paḷḷikaḷukku varum viṇṇappaṅkaḷaip pārttāl mayakkam vantuviṭum. āṅkilattil ciṟappu nilaiyil 80% mēl eṇ vāṅkiya oruvar eḻutiya viṇṇappattil kuṟaintatu aintāṟu cātāraṇap piḻaikaḷ irukkum. appaṭiyē tamiḻ payiṉṟavarkaḷ nērmukat tērvil caṅkap pāṭalkaḷil cātāraṇa kēḷvikaḷ kēṭkum pōtukūṭa, patil colla muṭiyātu tiṇaṟuvārkaḷ ittakaiya āciriyarkaḷiṭam pāṭam payilum māṇavarkaḷ eppaṭi vallunarkaḷāka-nāṭṭiṉ perumaiyaik kaṭṭikkāppavarāka varamuṭiyum? nāṭāḷum nallavar inta nilaiyiṉai eṇṇip pārkkavēṇṭum.

nāṭṭil poṟiyiyal kallūri, maruttuvak kallūri pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām nūṟṟukku 70, allatu 80 vāṅka

vēṇṭum eṉa varaiyaṟukkiṟārkaḷ, ‘pi.pi.ē.’ pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷukku 60% tēvaiyākiṟatu. āṉāl āciriyar paṭippukkō oṉṟum tēvai illai. pala muṟai tōlviyuṟṟu nūṟṟukku muppattaintu nāṟpatu vāṅkiṉāl pōtum eṉṟa viti uḷḷatu. ‘itu muṟaiyō! itu takumō! itu tarumantāṉō?’ eṉṟu vaḷḷalāraip pōṉṟu vāy viṭṭu alaṟa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.

āciriyar payiṟci ciṟuvakuppukaḷukku uriya āciriyarkaḷukku (Sec-Grade) iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ irukka, uyarnilaip paḷḷiyil payiṟṟuvōrukku ōrāṇṭākak kuṟaittatu ēṉō eppaṭi anta ellaiyil avarkaḷ payiṟci peṟa muṭiyum? avarkaḷukku (Practical work) kaḷappaṇiyum cemmaiyāka naṭaipeṟuvatillai. jūlai kaṭaiciyil cērntu, pipravari iṟutiyil muṭittu. mārccil tērvu eḻuti, paṭṭam peṟṟavar eppaṭi mutirnta aṟivuṭaiya pavaṇanti kāṭṭiya āciriyarāka muṭiyum? atilum ippōtu añcal vaḻikkalvi muṟaiyilē ippayiṟciyaip palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ pōṭṭiyiṭṭup pukuttiyuḷḷaṉa. eppaṭi, kaḷappaṇiyum pāṭaṅkaḷ aṟiyum nilaiyum avarkaḷukku amaiyum. ākavē eppaṭiyō iyantira cālaiyil iyantiraṅkaḷai uṟpatti ceytu aṉuppuvatu pōṉṟu palkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷ pallāyiravarai aṉuppi nāṭṭaip pāḻpaṭuttukiṟatu. iyantiraṅkaḷāvatu cemmaiyākac ceyalāṟṟum. āṉāl āciriyarkaḷ? ivarkaḷ tērviṉait takka vakaiyil 70% mēṟpaṭṭa eṇ peṟṟavarākat tērnteṭuttu, iraṇṭāṇṭu takka āciriyariṉ kīḻp payiṟci peṟac ceytāl ivarkaḷiṭam payilvōr ciṟantu viḷaṅkuvar. inta aṭippaṭai illai. nāṭṭil kalviyiṉ taram ‘kuṟaintu viṭṭatu-kuṟaintu viṭṭatu’ eṉṟu mattiya amaiccarkaḷ-mānila mutalvarkaḷ ākiyōr kūṟuvatil payaṉillai. cikareṭṭu viḷamparattai aḻakāka nāḷitaḻil viḷamparappaṭutti, aṭiyil cikareṭṭu piṭippatu nōyaṟṟa vāḻvukkum kēṭu payappatu’ eṉṟu acciṭa vēṇṭum eṉa: aracāṅkam caṭṭam ceytuḷḷatu. cikareṭṭai aracāṅkam virumpiṉāl taṭai ceyyalām. ataic ceyyātu, atu paṟṟi viḷamparaṅkaḷaiyum aṉumatittu, ataṉ kīḻē atu tīṅku ceyyak kūṭiyatu eṉṟu acciṭuvatu kēlik kūttāka allavō uḷḷatu. nāṭṭu makkaḷ aṉaivarum muṭṭāḷkaḷ eṉak karutukiṟārkaḷō eṉavum niṉaikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. eṉavē inta vakaiyil nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇāṉa kalviyiṉaik kāṭṭum nallāciriyarkaḷait tērnteṭukkum mutal paṇi cemmaiyāka naṭaipeṟa vēṇṭum. attakaiya nilai amaintāl-nallavar-kaṟṟavar-vallavar-kaṭamai uṇarntu ceyalāṟṟupavar (kai eḻuttiṭṭu vēṟu vēlai kavaṉikkātu ceyalpuripavar) māṇavar nalam karuti avar uḷaṅkoḷap pāṭam colpavar pavaṇantiyiṉ ilakkaṇattukku ilakkiyamāka amaipavar āciriyarkaḷāka amaivar. avvāṟu amaintāl, nāṭṭuk kalvi uyarnilai peṟum eṉpatu uṟuti. āṭciyāḷarkaḷ itil mutal kavaṉam-mukkiya kavaṉam celuttavēṇṭum.

palkalaik kaḻakamāṉiyak kuḻu, inta añcal vaḻikkalvi muṟaiyiṉai-āciriyar payiṟci aḷavil eṭuttuviṭap pōvatāka aṇmaiyil oru ceyti vantāka aṟikiṟēṉ: viraivil ceyalākkap peṟiṉ payaṉ viḷaiyum.

maṟṟoru vēṭikkai. kallūri āciriyarukku" entavitap payiṟciyum illai. nēṟṟuvarai ‘em.ē.’, payiṉṟavar em.pil., tērvu eḻutiyavar, iṉṟu cūṉ tiṅkaḷil anta ‘em.ē’ allatu ‘pi.ē’. vakuppiṟkup pāṭam kaṟpikkum āciriyarākiviṭalām. itai enta aṟivuḷḷa ulakamāvatu ēṟṟukkoḷḷumā? eṇṇippārkka vēṇṭāmā? piṉ evvāṟu nam māṇavarkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum? iṉiyākilum uḷḷa āciriyap payiṟcik kallūriyilō aṉṟip putitākak kallūri amaittō taṉiyākak kallūri āciriyarkaḷukkup payiṟciyiṉai aḷittal iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum, paḷḷikaḷum kallūrikaḷum nāṭṭil palkip peruki. viṭṭaṉa. āṉāl taram tāḻntuviṭṭatu. itutāṉ āḷum talaivarkaḷ uṭpaṭap palarum kūṟuvatu. ēṉ inta avalanilai? nāṉ mēlē kāṭṭiya iraṇṭoṉṟu maṭṭum kāraṇamāka niṉṟuviṭātu. iṉṉum cila uḷḷaṉa.

iṉṟu ellārum kalvi kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉṟa avāviṉāl palarum tam kuḻantaikaḷaip paḷḷikku aṉuppukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl nakaraṅkaḷilum - kirāmaṅkaḷilum kūṭa āṅkilp payiṟcip paḷḷikaḷē atikamāka vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. ‘vīti’tōṟum iraṇṭoru paḷḷi eṉṟu pārati ittakaiya paḷḷikaḷaic collavillai. vīṭṭil peṟṟōrkaḷum mammi, ṭāṭi eṉṟu kuḻantaikaḷ aḻaippatil perumai koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. āyirakkaṇakkil ilañcam koṭuttu, nūṟṟukkaṇakkil mātac campaḷam kaṭṭi, nerukkamāṉa vīṭukaḷilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa ippaḷḷikaḷil peṟṟōr tam kuḻantaikaḷaic cērkkiṉṟaṉar. aṅkē uriya pāṭaṅkaḷaiyum naṉku kaṟṟut taruvatillai. eṅkō oru cila paḷḷikaḷ tavira, pala, vaṇika nilaiyaṅkaḷākavē ceyalpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟait taṭukka aracāṅkam takka naṭavaṭikkai eṭukka vēṇṭum. attakaiya paḷḷikaḷil payiṉṟa piḷḷaikaḷai ekkāraṇam koṇṭum, aracāṅka icaivu peṟṟa paḷḷikaḷil cērkkak kūṭātu eṉṟa uttiraviṭṭu, ataic cemmaiyākap pātukākka vēṇṭum. ‘mutaṟkōṇal muṟṟum kōṇal’ eṉṟapaṭi inta mutaṟ kōṇalē nāṭṭuk kalviyai nalivu ceykiṟatu. itu māṟṟap peṟal vēṇṭum. aracāṅkam uṭaṉ āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum.

paḷḷiyil payilum māṇavarum āciriyarum oruvarai oruvar purintukoḷḷum vakaiyil pāṭaṅkaḷ amaivatōṭu avaṟṟai māṇavar naṉku aṟintu teḷinta piṉṉarē mēl vakuppiṟku māṟṟap peṟal vēṇṭum. paḷḷiyil cērntuviṭṭāl pattām vakuppu varai, taṉṉai yārum taṭai ceyya muṭiyātu eṉṟa niṉaivil palar paṭikkāmalum paḷḷikkup pala nāḷ varāmalum vēṟu vakaiyil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. cila peṟṟōr taṅkaḷ vēlaikaḷukkup piḷḷaikaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.

ovvoru vakuppilum tērviṉai muṟaiyāka naṭatti naṉku eḻuti veṟṟi peṟupavaraiyē mēl vakuppiṟku aṉuppa vēṇṭum, entavitat tērvum iṉṟip pattām vakuppu varaiyil cellalām eṉṟa nilai orukāl iruntatu. nūṟṟukkup patiṉaintu peṟṟāl pōtum; mēl vakuppiṟkup pōkalām eṉṟa niyatiyum orukāl iruntatu. iṉṟu avai illai eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ. paḷḷiyil payilum piḷḷaikaḷ vakuppiṟku ēṟṟa pāṭaṅkaḷai muṟaiyākap payiṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāka mēlēṟiṉāl tāṉ payaṉ uṇṭu. inta nilai naṉku vaṟpuṟuttappaṭa vēṇṭum.

āṇṭutōṟum āciriyar nāḷ (cep-5) naṭaipeṟukiṟatu tērccipeṟṟa ciṟanta māṇavarkaḷukkup paricuvaḻaṅkap peṟukiṟatu. nallāciriyar virutum vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṉṟatu. ‘nallāciriyar’ virutu paṟṟic cilarukku maṉakkacappu uṇṭu. iṅkē nāṉ atupaṟṟi ārāyavirumpavillai. āṉāl māṇavarukkup paricu vaḻaṅkuvatu eṇṇattakkatu. pala paḷḷikaḷil māṇavar taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ paḷḷikaḷilēyē tērvu eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. pala paḷḷikkūṭa māṇavarkaḷ vēṟupaḷḷikaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ kāraṇattai nāṉ ārāya vēṇṭā. āṉāl tam paḷḷiyil tērvu eḻutum māṇavarkaḷukku āciriyarkaḷ tāmē viṭaicollit taruvatum, nūlkaḷait tantu viṭai eḻuta vaippatum cilaviṭaṅkaḷil nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl atē paḷḷiyil tērvu eḻutum vēṟṟuppaḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukku viṉāttāḷum uriya vēḷaiyil taruvatillai muṭiyap pattu nimiṭam irukkum pōtu, eḻutat tāḷkēṭṭāl, ‘nī iṉi eṉṉa eḻutik kiḻikkappōkiṟāy! pōtum koṭu’ eṉṟu taṭuppavarum uḷḷaṉar. itupōṉṟa koṭumaikaḷpaṟṟit tērvāṇaiyarukkum peṟṟōr kaṭitam eḻuti iruppārkaḷ.

muṉpellām oru paḷḷimāṇavar maṟṟoru paḷḷiyil tērvu eḻutuvatu

vaḻakkam. tiru vi ka. paḷḷi kiṟittuvar kallūrip paḷḷiyil eḻutalām. kiṟittuvar kallūrip paḷḷi vēṟupaḷḷiyil eḻutalām. vēṟu paḷḷi māṇavar tiru.vi.ka. vil eḻutalām. inta muṟai kallūrikkum poruntum. nāṉ paccaiyapparil paṇiyāṟṟiya ñāṉṟu perumpālum layōlā kallūri māṇavar paccaiyapparil eḻutuvar.

inta muṟai iruppiṉ māṇavar uṇmaiyāṉa tiṟaṉ kaṇṭu teḷiyap peṟum. illaiyāyiṉ iṉṟu ciṟanta māṇavarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkum paricai ‘lañcamaṟṟu’ vaḻaṅkiya paricākakkoḷḷa iyalātu. mattiya kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷil vēṟu paḷḷiyiṉ talaivar vantu paḷḷiyiṉ tērvai naṭattuvar. ataṉiṉum muntiyatu ciṟantatu. vēṇṭumāyiṉ māṇavar aṭaiyāḷam kāṭṭa, payiṉṟa paḷḷiyiṉ iraṇṭoru āciriyar tērvuppaḷḷiyil kaṇkāṇippāḷarākac cellalām. oru kallūriyil mūṉṟumaṇi nērat tāḷai nāṉkumaṇi nēram eḻutavaittatōṭu viṭaikaḷaiyum āciriyarkaḷ collikkoṭuttārkaḷām. eppōtō oru muṟai palkalaik kaḻakap paṟakkumpaṭai varum. appōtu caṟṟu nimirntu viṭuvārkaḷām. itupaṟṟic ceṉṟa āṇṭu palkalaikkaḻakattukkuk kuṟippuvantatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl palkalaikkaḻakam eṉṉa naṭavaṭikkai eṭuttatō teriyātu. eṉavē, tērvumuṟai intavakaiyil māṟṟi amaittālaṉṟi, ciṟanta māṇavarukkup paricu vaḻaṅkal oru kēlikkūttākavē muṭiyum.

iṉi paḷḷinaṭakkum vēḷaiyiṉaippaṟṟi eṇṇuvōm. mēlaināṭukaḷilum amerikkā jappāṉ malēciyā pōṉṟa kīḻaināṭukaḷilum paḷḷikaḷ kālai 7.30 allatu 8kkuḷ toṭaṅki 12 allatu 1 maṇikkuḷ muṭintuviṭum. kālai 8 maṇikkuc cūriyaṉaik kāṇāta anta nāṭukaḷilellām avvāṟu viṭiyalil paḷḷikaḷ naṭakkumpōtu, namnāṭṭil maṭṭum pattukku eṉa vaittu, naṉku uṇṭapiṟaku, uṟaṅkum nilaiyil paḷḷikkup piḷḷaikaḷai aṉuppukiṟōm. ceṉṉai pōṉṟa nakaraṅkaḷ tāy, tantaiyar iruvarum vēlaikkup pōvatāl perumpālum avarkaḷpaṇi 10kkut toṭaṅkuvatāl kuḻantaikaḷai appaṭiyē paḷḷiyil viṭṭu vēlaikkuc cella ceḷakariyamākum eṉa niṉaikkiṉṟaṉar. nallavēḷaiyāka, pas nerukkaṭi kāraṇamākac ceṉṟa āṇṭu kālai 8.30kkup paḷḷikaḷ toṭaṅka muṭivu ceytu iṉṟu naṭaimuṟaiyil uḷḷatu. inta nilai toṭarntu naṭaipeṟutal nallatu. .

‘veḷḷi muḷaikkap paḷḷikkum vārum’ eṉṟu vināyakar vaḻipāṭṭil ōraṭi varukiṟatu. appaṭi viṭiyaṟkālaiyil ceṉṟu. viḷakkiṉṟēṉum muṟaiyam collum vakaiyil, pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷai, vāypāṭukaḷai muṟaiyāka āciriyarō, maṟṟaiya aṟinta māṇavarō colla, maṟṟavarkaḷ colluvārkaḷ. piṉ poḻutu viṭiya; avaravar vīṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu, oṉpatu maṇi aḷavil vantu pāṭaṅkaḷaip payilvar. inta ‘muṟaiyam’ collum vakaiyil kīḻvāy ilakkam, mutaliya kaṇakkukkuriya vāypāṭukaḷ maṉappāṭamākiṉṟamaiyāl iṉṟaiya (Clerk Table) kiḷārkṭēpil pōṉṟavai illāmalē ettakaiya. piṉṉa kaṇakkukaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum maṉattālēyē pōṭṭu muṭivu eṭuppar. appaṭiyē nikaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa paṭippatāl, akarāti iṉṟiyē ellā ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkum urai kāṇpar. nalla ilakkiyaṅkaḷ maṉappāṭam āvatāl avartam pēccum eḻuttum ciṟantu viḷaṅkum. iḷamaiyil eṉ kirāmattil, akkālattil (1918-24) ivvāṟu payiṉṟamai iṉṟum niṉaivukku varukiṟatu. āyiṉum inta muṟai iṉṟu naṭaipeṟātu. atupōṉṟu viṭiyalil eḻuntu collittarum āciriyarum illai; māṇvarkaḷum eḻuntu pōka māṭṭārkaḷ. ōraḷavu iṉṟu vēṟupala cātaṉaṅkaḷ vantuviṭṭamaiyāl maṉappāṭam tēvai illāta oṉṟāki viṭṭatu maṉitaṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa kaṇippoṟi maṉitaṉaik kāṭṭilum āyiramāyiram niṉaivāṟṟal uṭaiyatāka, maṉitaṉuṭaiya vāḻvaiyē taṉatākkik koḷḷum nilaiyil nām vāḻkiṟōm. eṉavē kurukula vācamum maṉappāṭam ceyyum muṟaiyum mella mella illaiyākiviṭṭaṉa.

iṉi, pāṭaṅkaḷaip paṟṟi ōraḷavu eṇṇalām eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.



kaṇitam paṉṉiraṇṭāṇṭu payilvar piṉ kārkoḷvāṉilōr mīṉ nilaitērntilār





aṇicey kāviyam āyiram kaṟkiṉum āḻntirukkum kaviyuḷam kāṇkilār





vaṇikamum poruḷ nūlum pitaṟṟuvār vāḻum nāṭṭil poruḷkeṭal kēṭṭilār





tuṇiyum āyiram cāttira nāmaṅkaḷ colluvār eṭṭuṇaip payaṉkaṇṭilār





eṉṟupārati pāṭiya atē nilaiyiltāṉē iṉṟum nāṭu irukkiṟatu. 11+2+2 eṉpatumāṟi 11+1+3 eṉamāṟiṟṟu piṉ atuvum māṟi 10+2+3 eṉa māṟiṟṟu. ippaṭi varuṭak kaṇakku māṟi māṟi varuvatāl maṭṭum kalviyiṉ taram uyarntu viṭumā? pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷil eṉṉa māṟṟam uṇṭu? appaṭiyē tamiḻ iraṇṭām iṭattiliruntu mutaliṭam eṉṟu peyaraip peṟṟatē tavira āṅkilamtāṉ kaṭṭāyam payila vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. tamiḻō piṟa intiya moḻikaḷōṭu, maṟṟaiya pirañcu, lattīṉ moḻikaḷōṭu oru moḻiyākavē otukkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil tiru. aviṉāciliṅkam ceṭṭiyār kalvi amaiccarāka iruntapōtu, iṉṟaikkuc cumār nāṟpatāṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil payilum māṇavar aṉaivarum tamiḻaik kaṭṭāyamākap paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉa vaṟpuṟuttiṉar. caṭṭam ceytār. aṉṟu pāṭa nūlkaḷait taṉiyār veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. eṉiṉum attakaiya pāṭanūlkaḷai uṭaṉ tuṇintu eḻuta yārum muṉ varavillai immuṟai eṉṉākumō? eṉa añciṉar. amaiccar eṉṉai aḻaittu eḻutaccoṉṉārkaḷ. nāṉ pāṭanūl eḻutuvatu illai eṉṟa koḷkai uṭaiyavaṉ. eṉiṉum avar kēṭṭukkoṇṭataṟkāka icaintu āṟu, ēḻu. eṭṭu ākiya mūṉṟu vakuppukaḷukkup pāṭanūl eḻutiṉēṉ. ataṉai veḷiyiṭat tamiḻnāṭṭup patippakaṅkaḷ viraintu ceyalpaṭātapōtu, appōtu āṅkilap patippakamāka irunta mākmillaṉ kampeṉiyē veḷiyiṭṭatu. piṉṉāl palarum veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.

tamiḻiṉaip potu, ciṟappu eṉa iruvakaiyākap pirittu, potuviṉai ellārum payilavēṇṭum eṉavum ciṟappiṉukkup patilāka ciṟupāṉmaiyōr avaravar tāy moḻiyiṉaiyō vēṟu vēṇṭiya moḻiyiṉaiyō kaṟkalām eṉavum vitittu avvāṟē oru cila āṇṭukaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻ kaṭṭāyamāka iruntatu. atē vēḷaiyil vēṟṟu moḻiyāḷarākiya ciṟupāṉmaiyōrukkum avaravar moḻiyiṉaik kaṟka vāyppum iruntatu. āyiṉum atē kāṅkiraciṉaic cērnta tiru. cuppiramaṇiyam kalviyamaiccarāka vantatum, anta muṟaiyai eṭuttuviṭṭu, tamiḻaip patiṉāṉku moḻikaḷōṭu oṉṟākkiṉār. aṉṟumutal varum kalvi amaiccarkaḷ mutalvarkaḷuṭaṉ itaic ceyalākkavēṇṭum; tamiḻ nāṭṭil tamiḻaik kaṭṭāyamākka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭum yārum cevicāykkavillai. aṇṭai mānilaṅkaḷil kaṉṉaṭam, marātti pōṉṟa moḻikaḷ kaṭṭāyamāka vantu ettaṉaiyō āṇṭukaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉa. nalla vēḷai iṉṟaiya mutalvarum kalvi amaiccarum viraivil tamiḻaik kaṭṭāyam payila vaḻikāṇa ēṟpāṭu ceyvatāka

aṟiya āṟutal piṟakkiṟatu. avarkaḷai vāḻttiyum pārāṭṭiyum kaṭitaṅkaḷ eḻutiṉēṉ. viraintu ceyvārkaḷ eṉa nampukiṟēṉ. aṭutta kalviyāṇṭilēyē (1992-93) atu ceyalākkappeṟum eṉa nampukiṟēṉ.

pāṭanūlkaḷai iṉṟu aracāṅkamē veḷiyiṭukiṟatu. anta veḷiyīṭṭakam periya pattu māṭik kaṭṭaṭamum kaṭṭikkoṇṭu ciṟakka vāḻkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum pāṭaṅkaḷaip payiṟṟum āciriyarkaḷukkum māṇavarkaḷukkum nalla nūlkaḷait tērnteṭukkum vāyppu illai. virumpiṉālum virumpāviṭṭālum orē vakaiyāṉa nūlaittāṉ paṭikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. muṉpellām-kālnūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ pala patippāḷarkaḷ-nūṟṟukku mēl iruppar eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ. moḻippāṭaṅkaḷ uṭpaṭa nalla pāṭanūlkaḷai eḻutiṉārkaḷ-veḷiyiṭṭārkaḷ. pala teḷinta aṟivum matinuṭpamum uṭaiya āciriyarkaḷ nūlkaḷ eḻuta vāyppu iruntatu. appaṭiyē patippakattārum taramāṉa nūlkaḷai veḷikkoṇara muṭintatu. avaṟṟai ārāya ovvoru pāṭattiṟkum valluṉarkuḻukkaḷ amaittu, nallaṉavaṟṟaik koṇṭu, allaṉavaṟṟait taḷḷiṉar. paḷḷikaḷilum nallaṉavaṟṟai ārāynteṭuttu, ciṟakkak kalviyiṉai-ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷaiyum kaṟṟut tantaṉar. ippōtō āḷum kaṭcikku vēṇṭiyavarkaḷai āciriyar kuḻukkaḷāka amaittu avarkaḷ tērnteṭukkum pakutikaḷai-cilacamayam araciyal vāṭai vīcuvaṉavāka uḷḷavaṟṟaiyum tokuttup pāṭanūlkaḷākat tarukiṉṟaṉa. cila camayam pāṭattiṭṭattiṉaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum cariyākap purintu koḷḷātu ētō tokuttōm eṉṟu pāṭanūlkaḷait tokuttuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. putiya pāṭattiṭṭam (10+2) vantapōtu oru pāṭattiṟku ivvāṟu tavaṟāka acciṭappeṟṟa cumār 40000 nūlkaḷ (nāṟpatāyiram) taḷḷappeṟṟu vēṟu putiya vakaiyil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭateṉak kēḷvippaṭṭēṉ. (appōtu uyarnilaip paḷḷikkuḻuvil nāṉum iṭam peṟṟiruntēṉ),

taṉiyār vacam oppuvittāl cilacamayaṅkaḷil tavaṟu uṇṭākiṟatu eṉpār kūṟṟu kātil viḻukiṉṟatu. vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ-allatu kaiyūṭṭu atikamākat tarukiṉṟavarkaḷ allatu mēliṭattu ‘cipāricu’ peṟṟavarkaḷ pāṭanūlkaḷai -avai piḻaipaṭṭaṉavāyiṉum veḷiyiṭa opputal taruvatum, attakaiya nūlkaḷai appaṭiyē kaiyūṭṭu mutaliyaṉa peṟṟu paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷil vaikkappeṟuvatum uṇṭu eṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nāmum avartam kūṟṟiṉaiyum nūṟṟukku nūṟupoy eṉṟu taḷḷavum muṭiyātu. palkalaikaḻakap pāṭanūl kuḻuviṉar inta vakaiyil ceyalpaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa pēccu aṭikkaṭi kātil viḻukiṟatu. inta āṇṭu oru palkalaikkaḻakattil oru tamiḻppakuti nīkkappeṟṟu vēṟu amaikka ēṟpāṭu ceyyappeṟṟatai nāḷitaḻkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭaṉa. eṉavē kuṟai etilum illāmal illai. eṉiṉum palavaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟait terinteṭuttu nallaṉa ceytu vaḻikāṇa vēṇṭuvatu aracāṅkattiṉ kaṭamaiyākum-iṉṟiyamaiyātatumākum.

perumpālum uyarnilai, mēlnilaippaḷḷippāṭaṅkaḷait tokukkum poṟuppiṉaik kallūri āciriyarkaḷiṭam viṭukiṉṟatu. itu eṇṇippārkka vēṇṭiya oṉṟu. kallūriyil āciriyap payiṟci peṟāta āciriyarkaḷ koḷvōr koḷkai aṟintu, iḷam uḷḷaṅkaḷ aṟintu pāṭaṅkaḷai amaikka iyalātu eṉpatu palar karuttu. mattiya paḷḷikaḷil 10,12 ākiya tērvu eḻutum vakuppukaḷ tavirttu muṟṟaiya aṉaittu vakuppukaḷukkum taṉiyār pāṭanūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa-taramāka uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē payilpavar ciṟakka varukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa nilaiyum uṇṭākiṟatu. itai māṟṟuvatil tavaṟu illai. aṟiviyal pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷai vēṇṭumāyiṉ attakaiya uyarnilaiyil uḷḷavar iraṇṭoruvar tuṇaikoṇṭu veḷiyiṭalām. āṉāl moḻippāṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavai payiṟṟupavarkaḷil aṉupavam mutirntavarkaḷaik koṇṭu tokukkappeṟiṉ payaṉ viḷaiyum.

aṇmaiyil mattiya aracāṅkam karumpalakait tiṭṭam eṉṟa oru tiṭṭattil ārampappaḷḷikaḷil payilum māṇavarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakaiyil kōṭikkaṇakkil vārivaḻaṅkiyatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil antat tokaiyiṉait takka vakaiyil-akkuḻantaikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakaiyil nūlkaḷait tērnteṭukkavillai eṉṟu cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭic cila itaḻkaḷ kaṇṭaṉam terivittaṉa. āṉāl atai maṟuttu aracāṅkamō anta nūlkaḷukku uriyavarkaḷō enta maṟuppum veḷiyiṭāta kāraṇattāl akkuṟṟaccāṭṭukaḷ uṇmai eṉṟu koḷḷattāṉē vēṇṭi uḷḷatu. eṉavē ittakaiya kuṟaipāṭukaḷ illāvakaiyil māṇavartam etirkāla vāḻkkaikkup payaṉpaṭum vakaiyil nalla pāṭa nūlkaḷai veḷikkoṇara muyalavēṇṭum eṉak kēṭṭukkoḷkiṟēṉ. ovvoru vakuppiṟkum amaikkum pāṭaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiyum piṟavaṟṟaip paṟṟiyum antantat tuṟaikaḷait taṉiyākak kāṇumpōtu viḷakkalām eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

tāymoḻiyilēyē kalvi amaiyavēṇṭum eṉṟu viṭutalaikku muṉpē makkaḷ kiḷarnteḻuntaṉar. irājāji mutalvarāka irunta kālattil ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷukkum nalla tamiḻccoṟkaḷaik kaṇṭu taṉittaṉi (ovvoru pāṭattiṟkum) akarāti nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻil eḻutuvōrkku ūkkam aḷittār irājāji. ataṟkup piṟakum orumuṟai inta vakaiyil ellāp pāṭaṅ kaḷukkum taṉittaṉi akarātikaḷ vantaṉa. mattiya aracum oru moḻikku oru kōṭi aḷavil avvam moḻiyil pāṭa

ka—2 nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭuvataṟkeṉa māṉiyamākat tantu ūkkiyatu. piṟa mānilaṅkaḷellām avaṟṟaic celavu ceytu nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭaṉa. cila mānilaṅkaḷ koṭutta māṉiyam pōtātu eṉṟu mēlum kēṭṭaṉa. āṉāl tamiḻakam taṉakkeṉa māṉiyamākat tantatil peruntokaiyiṉait tirumpat tantatu eṉak kūṟakkēṭṭirukkiṟēṉ. ‘tamiḻ’ ‘tamiḻ’ eṉṟu mēṭaiyil pēcuvatē aṉṟic ceyalil kāṭṭuvar ilarē eṉa varuntavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu iṉṟu (I.A.S.) ai.ē.es. mutaliya tērvukaḷil tamiḻnāṭu eṅkēyō piṉtaṅkiviṭṭatu eṉa araciyal talaivarkaḷum āḷvōrum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. oru kālattil mutaliṭattil irunta tamiḻakam iṉṟu kaṭai iṭattil varakkāraṇm eṉṉa? pāṭaṅkaḷai avvam moḻiyil payilalām eṉṟa nilai maṟṟa mānilaṅkaḷil iruppatōṭu ai.ē.es. tērviṉaiyum avaravar tāymoḻiyil eḻutalām eṉṟa vitiyum uḷḷatu. vaṭanāṭṭavarum piṟarum intiyilum piṟa moḻikaḷilum eḻuti avvam moḻi aṟintavarālēyē tiruttappeṟṟu nalla iṭaṅkaḷaip peṟukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruntu ai.ē.es. eḻutukiṉṟavarkaḷ ettaṉai pēr tamiḻil eḻutukiṉṟaṉar? yār eṇṇip pārppavar?

kāl nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ ai.ē.es. tērviṟkut tamiḻil orutāḷ amaikka virumpiyapōtu, nāṉum tiru te.po. mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār avarkaḷum pāṭat tiṭṭam vakuttu oru tāḷai eḻuta ēṟpāṭu ceytōm. avarkaḷ mutal iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷum nāṉ piṉ iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷum tērvāḷarāka iruntōm. mikak kuṟaivākavē -oru cilarē eḻuta muṉvantaṉar. iṉṟum antat tāḷ irukkiṟatu eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ. (tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu-moḻi varalāṟu). atai eḻutupavarkaḷ uḷarō illaiyō eṉpatu teriyātu.

eṉavē tamiḻaṉ pēcciṉai viṭuttut tāymoḻiyil kalviyaik kaṟṟut teḷintālaṉṟi, pārata nāṭṭup piṟa pakutikaḷōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭa muṭiyātu. iṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭil māvaṭṭa āṭciyil pātikkumēl tamiḻ aṟiyātavarkaḷākavē uḷḷamaikkum, kōṭṭaiyil pala ai.ē.es. atikārikaḷ tamiḻ payilātavarkaḷāka iruppataṟkum ituvē kāraṇam. tilli mattiya aracāṅkattilum irupattaintu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ettuṟaiyil ceṉṟālum tamiḻaraip perumpālum kāṇamuṭiyum. avarkaḷum iṅkiruntu celvōrai aṉpuṭaṉ ēṟṟu āvaṉa ceytu utaviṉar. iṉṟu aṅkēyum tamiḻaṉaik kāṇpataritu. ēṉ inta avala nilai? itait tirutta vēṇṭāmā?

nāṉ āṅkilattaiyō intiyaiyō vēṟṟu moḻiyaiyō nam māṇavar kaṟkavēṇṭām eṉṟu collavillai. ‘tāyk kolaic cālpuṭaittu eṉpārum uṇṭu’ eṉṟu muṉṉaip periyavarkaḷ kūṟiyatu pōṉṟu tāymoḻiyaik kolaiceytuviṭṭu vēṟṟumoḻiyiṉai ‘ōmpu’tal tavaṟu eṉavē cuṭṭikkāṭṭiṉēṉ. tamiḻōṭu ettaṉai moḻi vēṇṭumāṉālum kaṟkalām tamiḻaṉ attuṇai aṟivum āṟṟalum tiṟaṉum peṟṟavaṉ. itai yārum maṟukkamuṭiyātu. āṉāl tamiḻukkut tamiḻnāṭṭil mutaliṭam taravēṇṭum eṉpatē eṉ vēṇṭukōḷ! itu putitaṉṟu. ippaṭiyē pala mānilaṅkaḷil-ulakil pala nāṭukaḷ uḷḷa nilaiyiṉai aṟiyavēṇṭum.

tamiḻnāṭṭil orucila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ āṅkilam payiṟṟuvikka aṭikkaṭi vitikaḷ māṟṟappeṟṟaṉa. orumuṟai āṟām vakuppil āṅkilam toṭaṅkiṉāl pōtum eṉṟum, oru muṟai mūṉṟu allatu nāṉkām vakuppil toṭaṅkiṉāl pōtum eṉṟum aracu āṇaiyiṭṭatāka aṟikiṟēṉ. antak kālattiltāṉ taṉiyār āṅkilap paḷḷikaḷ mūlaik koṉṟākat tōṉṟa ārampittaṉa. antō āṅkilam illaiyā? eṉṟu oṉṟum aṟiyāk kirāmavācikaḷ uḷpaṭa, āṅkilap paḷḷi eṅkē eṅkē eṉṟu tēṭi avaṟṟuḷ tam piḷḷaiyaic cērkkappāṭupaṭṭaṉar. iṉṟu kaṭṭuppāṭṭukku aṭaṅkā vakaiyil atu peruki viṭṭatu. avaṟṟai muṟaippaṭuttuvatum peṟṟōrkaḷait teḷivupaṭuttuvatum aracāṅkattiṉ kaṭamaiyākum.

tamiḻnāṭṭaip poṟutta vakaiyil 10ām vakuppu varaiyil palappala vakaiyil paḷḷikaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. aracāṅka icaivu peṟāta kuḻantaikaḷ paḷḷikaḷ, ārampap paḷḷikaḷ āyirakkaṇakkil perukiyuḷḷaṉa. piṉ āṟu mutal pattu varaiyilum ettaṉaiyō vēṟupāṭu! māṉiyam peṟum es.es.el.ci eṉum pattām vakuppu varai uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷ, āṅkilō intiyap paḷḷikaḷ, kīḻtticaip (Oriental) paḷḷikaḷ, mattiyap paḷḷikaḷ, tilli kiṟistava caṅkam naṭattum paḷḷikaḷ, meṭrikulēṣaṉ paḷḷikaḷ eṉap palavakaiyil paḷḷikaḷ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. mutalatu aracāṅka muḻu māṉiyam peṟṟu naṭaipeṟuvatu. āṅkilō intiyap paḷḷikaḷilō pāṭattiṭṭam-moḻit tiṭṭam mikak kuṟaivāka uḷḷatu. kīḻtticaip paḷḷikaḷum avvāṟē. mattiyap paḷḷikaḷum meṭrikulēṣaṉ paḷḷikaḷum āṅkilattaiyē payiṟṟu moḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. cila mattiyap paḷḷikaḷil inti payiṟci moḻiyāka irukkalām. ippaṭit tamiḻ nāṭṭu iḷañciṟuvarkaḷ payila iṭarppaṭum kalvi nilai tēvaitāṉā? aṉṟu āṅkilēyaṉ āṇṭakālattil āṅkilō intiyap paḷḷikaḷ, ammarapiṉaraic cārnta kuḻantaikaḷukkāka ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭirukkalām. iṉṟu anta marapiṉar tamiḻ marapiṉarōṭu oṉṟiviṭṭaṉarē! mēlum appaḷḷikaḷil 100kku 95kku mēl maṟṟavarkaḷ tāṉē payilkiṉṟaṉar. āciriyarkaḷum appaṭiyē. ciṟupāṉmai karutiyō vēṟu kāraṇattālō aṉṟu ārampittālum attakaiya nilai iṉṟu illaiyē! ciṟupāṉmaik kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷ aṉaittum anta nilaiyil tāṉē uḷḷaṉa. ciṟupāṉmai nilaiyaṅkaḷil 100kku 90kku mēl maṟṟavarkaḷtāṉē payilkiṉṟaṉar. kiṟittavap paḷḷikaḷilum ciṟupāṉmaiyōr naṭattum piṟa paḷḷikaḷilum, aṭippaṭaiyil amainta ciṟupāṉmaiyōr paḷḷikaḷilum payilvōruḷ 100kku 90kku mēl avvac ciṟupāṉmaiyaic cārātavarkaḷtāmē payilkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟaic ciṟupāṉmaiyōr kalviccālaikaḷ eṉak koṇṭu taṉic calukaikaḷ aḷippatu eppaṭip poruntum? avarkaḷē perumpāṉmaiyirākavō muṟṟumākavō payiṉṟāl taṉiccalukaikaḷ tēvaiyē! araciyal cācaṉattil itaṟku viḷakkam tarappeṟutal vēṇṭum.

eṅkō ceṉṟu viṭṭēṉ. maṉṉikkavum. tamiḻ nāṭṭil ivvāṟu palavakaik kalvikaḷ toṭakkanilaiyil iruppatu tēvaitāṉā! āṅkilap payiṟṟumoḻip paḷḷi kaḷil payilum māṇavar tāmē uyarntavar eṉṟu karutit taṉic cātiyāka vaḷarkiṉṟaṉar. uḷḷa vēṟupāṭu pōtātu eṉṟu intap putiya vēṟupāṭṭiṉai aracāṅkamē uṇṭākka vēṇṭumā? kalvittuṟai nirvākikaḷum amaiccakamum karuttirutti uṭaṉ itaṟku maruntu kāṇa vēṇṭum. kalviyai mānila ellaiyilē koḷḷa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, mattiya aracuṭaṉ iṇaittal tavaṟākum. itai uṇarum nāḷē nāṭṭil uṇmaiyāṉa kalvi malarum nāḷ. tamiḻnāṭṭu aracāṅkam uṭaṉ tīviramākac cintaṉai ceytu toṭakkak kalvi nilaiyil (10m vakuppu varai) ittaṉai vēṟupāṭukaḷ illaiyākac ceyyavēṇṭum. iḷam piñcu uḷḷaṅkaḷil kaṟkumpōtē māṟṟu eṇṇaṅkaḷai-vēṟupāṭṭu uṇarvukaḷai vaḷarkkum kalvi muṟai kaḷaiyappaṭal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl nakattāl kiḷḷuvatai viṭṭup piṉ kōṭari koṇṭu veṭṭa vēṇṭiya nilai uṇṭākum. inta aṭippaṭai māṟṟam ceyyātu, ettaṉai orumaippāṭu mānāṭukaḷ kūṭṭiṉālum vāṉoliyilum tolaikkāṭciyilum varicai varicaiyāka nikaḻccikaḷ amaittālum payaṉ viḷaiyātu. ārampak kalvi paṟṟiyum piṟa nilaikaḷaip paṟṟiyum uyarnilaik kalvi nilai mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiyum avvat talaippukaḷil piṟaku ārāyalām.

nam nāṭṭil naṉku payiṉṟu ciṟakkap paṭṭam peṟupavar nam nāṭṭil taṅkātu piṟa nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟuviṭukiṉṟārkaḷē eṉṟu peruntalaivarkaḷ uḷpaṭap palar varuntukiṉṟaṉar. aṇmaiyil nam tamiḻaka mutalvar kūṭa attakaiya karuttiṉai ōriṭattil pēciyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ. ataṟku aṭippaṭaik kāraṇattai ārāyavēṇṭum. nāṉ 1985il mēlai nāṭukaḷukkum amerikkāvukkum ceṉṟa kālai aṅkuḷḷavarkaḷai itupaṟṟik kēṭṭēṉ. pala nalla maruttuvarkaḷ (Doctors) poṟiyiyalāḷarkaḷ (Engineers)-piṟa tuṟaikaḷil vallunarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭaic cērntavarkaḷ-aṅkaṅkē ciṟakkac ceyal purintu avvan nāṭṭai vaḷappaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ovvoruvaraiyum nāṉ ‘tāynāṭṭai viṭṭu ēṉ vantīrkaḷ?’ eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ. avarkaḷ aṉaivarum kūṟiya patilkaḷ ēṟakkuṟaiya orē muṭiviṉait taruvatākavē uḷḷaṉa. ‘nāṅkaḷ iṅkē niṟaiyavē campātikkiṟōm. āṉāl tamiḻnāṭṭai viṭṭu vara maṉamillaitāṉ. iṅkē campātippatil pattil oru pākam aṅkē campātittālum pōtum; maṉa niṟaivōṭu vāḻvōm. iṅkē aṟivukku, tiṟaṉukku, āṟṟalukku, ākka vaḻikku uḻaikkum uḻaippukku mutaliṭam tarukiṟārkaḷ. aṅkēyō-tamiḻ nāṭṭil vēṇṭiyavarkaḷukku, kaiyūṭṭu tarupavarkaḷukku taṉ contakkāraṉukku-jātikkāraṉukkut tāṉē mutaliṭam. avarkaḷukkē patavi uyarvu, piṟa ellāc calukaikaḷum tarappeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu naintu pulampukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tiṟaṉāl aṅkē ettakaiya aṟputakaṅkaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ ellām-poṟiyāḷarkaḷ-maruttuvarkaḷ-nōpal paricu peṟṟavarkaḷ-pērāciriyarkaḷ-piṟa tuṟai vallunarkaḷ-iṅkē tamiḻnāṭṭil iruntāl nāṭu evvaḷavu muṉṉēṟum eṉa eṇṇi nāṉ varuntiya nāṭkaḷ pala. eṉ nūlil (ēḻu nāṭukaḷil eḻupatu nāṭkaḷ) itu paṟṟic ciṟitu kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. iṅkum palar uḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ kumuṟalōṭu vēlai ceyvataiyum aṟivēṉ. ataṉāl pala tuṟaikaḷil vēlaikaḷ mantamāvatōṭu muṟaiyaṟṟatākavum amaikiṉṟaṉa. kalvit tuṟaiyilēyē muṟaiyāka mēlē varavēṇṭiya oruvarait taḷḷi vēṟoruvarai uyarttiyatai avar collic collik kaṇṇīr viṭukiṟār eṉak kēḷvippaṭukiṟēṉ. inta nilai, nāṭṭil nīṅkiṉāl oḻiya, āyiram kōṭi kōṭiyāka nām kalvikkuc celaviṭum tokai yārukkō tāṉ payaṉpaṭuvakaiyil amaiyum, itai nam nāṭṭu aracu naṉku eṇṇi, takkatiṉṉa takātaṉa iṉṉa eṉṟu āyntu ceyalpaṭal naṉṟu.

nāṟpattaintāṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ kalvit tuṟai māvaṭṭak kaḻakattiṉkīḻ naṉku ceyalpaṭṭu vantatu. ‘District Board’ eṉṟum ‘Taluk Board’ eṉṟum māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ, vaṭṭaṅkaḷtoṟum āṭcip poṟuppai ēṟṟuc ceyalpurintaṉa. kalvi, cukātāram, cālai pōṉṟa mukkiya tuṟaikaḷ akkaḻakattiṉ vacam iruntaṉa. ataṉ uṟuppiṉar ovvoruvarum nāṟpatu allatu aimpatu kirāmattiṟku eṉat tērnteṭukkappaṭuvar. ovvoruvarum ciṟu aḷavil uḷḷa tam ellaiyiṉai aṭikkaṭi pārvaiyiṭṭu, makkaḷ tēvai aṟintu tiṅkaḷukku oru muṟai naṭakkum kaḻakak kūṭṭattil attēvaikaḷai vaṟpuṟutti makkaḷukku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic ceytuvantaṉar. atikāram ivvāṟu paravalāka iruntatōṭu, uṟuppiṉarum tam ellaiyaic cārntavarāka iruntatāl makkaḷum avarkaḷiṭam virumpic ceṉṟu tattam kuṟaikaḷai muṟaiyiṭuvar. maṟṟum ūrtoṟum uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷil āciriyarkaḷ ciṟakkap paṇiyāṟṟukiṉṟaṉarā eṉa mēṟpārvai pārkkavum vacatiyāka iruntatu. vaittiya vacatikaḷum cālai vacatikaḷum avvāṟē pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. kalvittuṟai neṟiyil māvaṭṭak kalvi aluvalar, vaṭṭa āyvāḷarkaḷiṉ nērmukap pārvaiyōṭu, ammāvaṭṭak kaḻaka uṟuppiṉar aṭikkaṭi paḷḷikaḷaip pārvaiyiṭṭu vantamaiyiṉ paḷḷikaḷ muṟaiyāka naṭaipeṟṟaṉa eṉalām. kallūri tavirttu maṟṟaiya ellāp paḷḷikaḷum ivarkaḷ mēṟpārvaiyilēyē iruntaṉa.

iṉṟō inta nilai illai. pañcāyattu oṉṟiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟu peyaraḷavil iruppiṉum ataṉ ellai virintuḷḷamaiyiṉ taṉi uṟuppiṉar nērmukap pārvai arukiviṭṭatu. muṉ iruntamai pōṉṟu oṉṟiyattukkeṉa, kalviyaik kavaṉittup pōṟṟa, taṉik kalvi aluvalakamum kiṭaiyātu. araciyal cūḻalāl aṭikkaṭi inta oṉṟiyaṅkaḷum kalaikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ūrtōṟum kalviyil muṉṉēṟṟam kāṇa muṭiyavillai eṅkiruntō āyvāḷar-muṉ aṟivippuṭaṉ varumpōtu eppaṭiyō vakuppukaḷaic carikaṭṭik kāṭṭiviṭṭup pala nāḷ mūṭik kiṭakkum paḷḷikaḷ pala uḷa. taṉiyāciriyar paḷḷikaḷaip paṟṟiyō colla vēṇṭiyatillai. takka nallāciriyarkaḷum taṟkālattil kiṭaippataritu. eṉavē kirāmap paḷḷikaḷil nalla muṟaiyil kalvi amaiya vēṇṭumāyiṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiya nallavar kuḻukkaḷ tēvai. iṅkuḷḷa piṟa nilaikaḷaippaṟṟip piṉpu taṉiyākak kāṇalām.

vayatu vantōrukku eṉak kalvi kaṟpikkum muṟai nāṭṭil pōṟṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. ataṟkeṉak kōṭik kaṇakkil mattiya aracum mānila aracum pōṭṭiyiṭṭuk koṇṭu celavu ceykiṉṟaṉa. pala taṉiyār niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷumkūṭa ōraḷavu utavukiṉṟaṉa. paḷḷi, kallūri māṇavarkaḷ itil īṭupaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum celavukku ēṟṟa payaṉ viḷaivatillai. ēṭṭuk kaṇakkiṉai nāṭṭu nikaḻcciyōṭu oppiṭṭup pārppiṉ uṇmai viḷaṅkum. taṟpōtu putiya vēkattil māvaṭṭantōṟum inta muyaṟci ceyalākkappeṟukiṉṟatu. ‘eḻuttaṟiyār inta māvaṭṭattil illaiyākac ceyvōm’ eṉṟu ovvoru māvaṭṭa āṭcit talaivarum avaraic cārntōrkaḷum muyalkiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum makkaḷ ottuḻaipput tēvai. ataip poṟuttiruntutāṉ kāṇa vēṇṭum. ellārum kaṟṟōrē eṉṟa nilai nāṭṭil malariṉ antanāḷ naṉṉāḷākum eṉpatu uṇmai! āṉāl annāḷ eṉṟu varumō!

ilavacak kalvi paṟṟiyum caṟṟu eṇṇip pārkkat tōṉṟukiṟatu. muṉpellām ārampappaḷḷi vakuppukaḷ aintām vakuppuvarai eṅkum ilavacamākavē iyaṅkiṉa. orucila taṉiyār-kiṟittavar paḷḷikaḷ pōṉṟavai mikak kuṟainta aḷavil campaḷam peṟṟaṉa. paḷḷiyil mattiya vakuppukaḷilum (6, 7, 8) mēl vakuppukaḷilum (9, 10, 11) mikak kuṟaivākavē campaḷam peṟṟaṉar. kallūrikaḷilum appaṭiyē. iṉṟu mēnilaippaḷḷi varaiyil kalvi ilavacamākkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. ‘itu tēvaitāṉā’ eṉṟa viṉā palar uḷḷattil eḻukiṉṟatu. ēḻaikaḷukku utava aṉṟum ceṉṉaik kalvi vitikaḷil 72il taṉiviti iruntatu. akkuṟainta campaḷattaiyum avarkaḷ kaṭṭavēṇṭiyatillai. appōtu irunta taram iṉṟu kalviyil illai. pala peṟṟōr paṇam celavillaiyātalāl piḷḷaikaḷ paṭippaip paṟṟik kavalaippaṭuvatillai. nāṭeṅkum ilavacap paḷḷikaḷ irukkumpōtu, nūṟum irunūṟum mātaccampaḷamum peṟṟu, cērkkumpōtu cila āyiraṅkaḷum naṉkoṭai vāṅkum piṟa paḷḷikaḷ vaḷarvataṟkuriya kāraṇattai eṇṇip pārkkavēṇṭum. pala ēḻaikaḷum kūṭa, piṟa celavukaḷaik kuṟaittukkoṇṭu campaḷam kaṭṭum paḷḷikaḷukkē piḷḷaikaḷai aṉuppak kāṇkiṟōm. ‘eṉavē ilavacak kalvi eṉil taram kuṟaintatu’ eṉṟu makkaḷ niṉaikkum aḷavukkut taṉiyār māṉiyam peṟum paḷḷikaḷum aracāṅkap paḷḷikaḷum ceyalpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru vakuppiṟku 40 allatu 45 eṉpatupōka 75 allatu 80 varai māṇavarai aṭaittuvaikkum avala nilaiyiṉaic cila paḷḷikaḷil kāṇkiṟōm. cūṉ mātam toṭaṅkiya paḷḷikku aṭutta āṇṭu pipravariyil tēvaiyāṉa āciriyarai aṉuppukiṉṟa aracāṅka vitimuṟaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. āciriyarkaḷum, muṉṉaiviṭap pala maṭaṅku-eṇṇip pārkka muṭiyāta aḷaviṟku-campaḷam vāṅkukiṟārkaḷ. eṉṟālum piḷḷaikaḷ kalviyil pōtiya karuttiṉaic celuttavillai eṉap peṟṟōrkaḷum, talaivarkaḷum, āḷunar, amaiccar pōṉṟārkaḷum collukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē ēḻaikaḷukku eṉat taṉic calukai aḷittuc campaḷam illaiyākki, iṉṟaiya nilaikkut tēvaiyāṉa campaḷattai ōrām vakuppu mutal varaiyaṟuttu vacūl ceytāl paṇam kaṭṭum peṟṟōrkkum akkaṟai uṇṭākum. araciṉ kaṇkāṇippum atikamākkappeṟal vēṇṭum. toṭakkak kalviyum aṭutta uyarnilaik kalviyum taram uyarntu takka vakaiyil māṇavarukku aḷikkappeṟṟāltāṉ piṉ kallūrik kalviyil teḷivum taramum kāṇamuṭiyum. eṉavē atikak kaṇkāṇippum kaṇṭippum ārampa uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷukkut tēvaiyāṉavai. ilavacak kalviyuṭaṉ araciyalum iṇaikkap peṟṟiruppatāl eṉ karuttiṉai ēṟṟukkoḷḷa māṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatu teriyum. eṉiṉum uḷḷa nilaiyiṉait teḷḷatteḷiyak kāṭṭavē itaik kuṟittēṉ.

ippaṭiyē ilavaca pas pās vaḻaṅkalum. itu vēṇṭumeṉṟu yārum kēṭkavillai. perumpālum paḷḷip piḷḷaikaḷ tattam vāḻiṭaṅkaḷukku arukil uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷilēyē iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉar. campaḷam vāṅkum paḷḷikaḷil perumpālaṉa untuvaṇṭikaḷ koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē inta nilai tēvaiyaṟṟatu. itaṉāl tēvai illātavarkaḷkūṭa iraṇṭoru niṟuttumiṭamāyiṉum ataṉ muṟṟiya ellai varaiyil ilavacaccīṭṭu vāṅki untu vaṇṭikaḷil nericalai uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum itait tayārippataṟkum takavalkaḷ cērttu aṉuppuvataṟkum pōkkuvarattu aluvalakamum ovvoru paḷḷiyum peṟum allalum avatiyum eḻuta muṭiyā! pala paḷḷikaḷil itaṉāl muṟaiyāṉa vēlaiyum taṭaippaṭukiṟatu. peṟṟōrkaḷum māṇavarkaḷum uṭaṉ vāṅkit taravillaiyē eṉa vātiṭum nilaiyum uṇṭākiṟatu. ippaṭit tēvaiyaṟṟa vakaiyil-araciyal piṉṉaṇiyil amaiyum cila ceyalkaḷaip paṟṟi atikam eḻutāmal iruppatē nalameṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

ituvarai kūṟiyavāṟṟāl tamiḻakak kalviyiṉ muntiya ēṟṟam, iṭaikkālap piṉṉaṭaivu, iṉṟaiya kuṟaikaḷ, paḷḷikaḷiṉ vakaikaḷ, piṟanāṭṭu nilaiyil kalvi amaippu mutaliyavaṟṟai ōraḷavu cuṭṭik kāṭṭiṉēṉ. nīkka vēṇṭiyavai paṟṟiyum ākka vēṇṭiyavai paṟṟiyum camutāya muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum tamiḻakam muṉpōṉṟu muṉ niṉṟu uyarnilai peṟutaṟkum vaḻittuṟaikaḷai ōraḷavu cuṭṭik kāṭṭiṉēṉ. iṉi ovvoru tuṟaiyiṉaiyum taṉittaṉiyē koṇṭu atatil uḷḷa nalakkēṭukaḷaiyum tēvaiyāṉa tiruttaṅkaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum paṟṟi ārāyalām eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

mēlum āciriyar nilai, māṇavar, peṟṟōr nilai, nāṭṭu naṭappu, aracāṅkattiṉ kaṭamai pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum uṭaṉ eṇṇip pārttalum ēṟpuṭaittākum eṉa niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ. toṭarntu nāṭṭuk kalvi nalam peṟṟu, ulaka araṅkil nam pāratamum ciṟappākat tamiḻakamum uyarveyti ulakōr pōṟṟum nilai eytavēṇṭum eṉṟa kuṟikkōḷuṭaṉēyē iṉi varuvaṉavaṟṟai eḻuta niṉaikkiṟēṉ. oruvaraiyum kuṟaicolluvataṟkeṉṟō kuṟṟam cāṭṭuvataṟkeṉṟō vēṟu paḻipaṭarnilaiyilō nāṉ ivaṟṟai eḻutavillai. ‘oṉṟē ceyyavum vēṇṭum, oṉṟum naṉṟē ceyyavum vēṇṭum’ eṉṟa aṭippaṭaiyil nāṭṭu nalaṉ-ataṉ kaṇṇākiya kalvi-atai makkaḷukku aḷikkum marapum muṟaiyum-ivai paṟṟiyē eṉ eṇṇamum eḻuttum paṟṟip paṭarum eṉakkūṟi mēlē cellukiṉṟēṉ.

nam nāṭṭuk kalvi vaḷarāmaikku intiya aracāṅkam amaitta kalvi āyvu paṟṟiyum tirutta vēṇṭiya muṟai paṟṟiyum viḷakka amaitta kuḻuviṉ amaippāḷar tiru. irāmamūrtti avartam muṉṉuraiyil kūṟiyavaṟṟai appaṭiyē uṅkaḷ muṉvaikkiṟēṉ.

One fundamental reason for failure has been that while we go on making radical protestations, our education to this day continues to be governed by the same assumptions, goals and values that governed it in the days of the British Raj. The British believed in the ‘downward filtration theory’ under which education and culture would inevitably flow from the classes to the masses. They kept the common people away from education. and education away from life. But things have not much changed since they left. Even today the principal beneficiaries of our education are the upper and middle classes. To them also we give a wrong education. Our formal system remains confined to the four walls of a school or college. It is tied down to text books and examinations. Even then the books are unreadable and the examinations totally unreliable, The courses of study are so framed that the students are not equipped with any productive skills. Whatever education they receive cuts them off from their natural and social environment. They become aliens to their own community. They lose faith in life itself what Jayaprakashji wrote in 1978 still holds true. According to him ‘it also converts them into a parasitic class which perpetuates and even intensifies the poverty of the masses. The system has failed to promote individual growth. It also becomes more of a hindrance than a help to bring about an egalitarian transformation’. If this be true, can we say that we have basically departed from the Macaulay tradition? And, if this is what our education has done to us, one may well ask, is not no education better than bad education?



One may admit—that for this situation education alone is not responsible, During the last forty-three years we have pursued a model of economic development that has led to the creation of two Indias—one of the rich, the other of the poor. A new privileged class has come into being. It holds monopoly over political and economic power and sources. of wealth. It controls culture and education. It is firmly established every where. It is this class whose interests our education is made to serve. The result is that as in economy so in education, two parallel systems have come into being– one for the rich, the other for the poor. No wonder, a divided education finds itself totally devitalised, and incapable of meeting the challenges of independent India's national life. To the rise and growth of this class, holding sway over the whole range of national affairs, can be traced most of the ills we are faced with— the erosion of social and moral values, weakening of democracy, the partisan character of our development, corruption and a number of other elitist aberrations. It is responsible for the impoverishment of the nation's very soul. It is, therefore time the nation, most of all education, took serious note of this phenomenon, and guarded against further damage to national life.

Report of the committee for Review of

National Policy of Education—1986
