mēṟku moḻikaḷ puviyicai ōṅkum’



eṉṟu 'kūṟat takātavaṉ kūṟiyatākat' tamiḻaṉṉai vāymoḻiyākavē kāṭṭuvar. iṉṟu:inta nilaiyiṉait tāṉē kāṇkiṟōm. mēlē āṟām vakuppilō vēṟu vakuppilō āṅkilam oru moḻiyāka āṅkilēyar kālattilē irunta nilaimāṟi-iṉṟu cutantiram peṟṟa nāṟpattaintu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu, anta āṅkilamē vīṭṭu moḻiyāka-vītiyil pēcum moḻiyāka-viti tōṟum uḷḷa paḷḷi moḻiyāka iyaṅkukiṟatu. itil tamiḻnāṭutāṉ muṉṉaṇiyil uḷḷatu eṉpar. veḷi nāṭṭār oruvar intiyāvaik kāṇa vantāl, 'iṅkē

teṟkē orumānilam āṅkilattiṉait tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷatu eṉak kēli ceyya māṭṭārā! pāratikku nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻā koṇṭāṭiṉōm. āṉāl avaṉ collai ēṟkātu, iḷaṅkuḻantaikaḷai-maḻalaikaḷai māṟṟu moḻi vaḻiyē vāḻa vaḻivakukkalāmā!

kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷil innilai mikavum kēvalamāki vantukoṇṭirukkiṟatu. āṉāl itilum piḷḷaikaḷ tiṟam peṟṟārkaḷā eṉṟāl illai. paḻaiya kālattil veḷḷaiyar vīṭṭil vēlai ceypavar-paṭlar iṅkiliṣ pēcuvār eṉpar atupōla veṟum pēccilē maṭṭumtāṉ anta āṅkilamoḻi. āḻnta aṟivilō eḻuttilō-paṭippilō antap paṭippiṉ nilai illai. oru kallūriyil āṅkilap pērāciriyarākap paṇiyāṟṟum oruvariṭam kārlail (Carlyle) eṉpāraip paṟṟit teriyumā eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ. avarkaḷ teriyātu eṉṟu talai āṭṭiṉar. cari kirē (Gray) eṉpāraip paṟṟit teriyumā eṉṟēṉ, patil illai. ṣēkspiyar, milṭaṉ, kōlṭusmit pōṉṟa paḻamperum āciriyarkaḷaip paṟṟiyō H. G. vels allatu parṉāṭṣā eṉṉum aṇmaiyil vāḻntu vāḻvilakkiyam pāṭiyavaraip paṟṟiyō aṟiyā nilaitāṉ kāṇkiṟatu. avarkaḷ eḻutum eḻuttilō oru varikku irupiḻaikaḷ kāṇalām. ippaṭit tāṉ palar uḷḷaṉar. cilar uṇmaiyilē vallavarāka irukkalām. āṉāl perumpālār kaṟṟum kalvātār. avarkaḷiṭamtāṉē māṇavarkaḷ payilkiṉṟārkaḷ. perumpālum attakaiyavartāmē iṅkē āciriyar patavikku varukiṟārkaḷ. cilar uḻaippiṉ payaṉāl ciṟitaḷavu muṉṉēṟukiṟārkaḷ eṉṟālum palar eṅkō ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē tamiḻk kalviyō-piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷō aṉṟi āṅkilak kalviyum. ittaṉai āravāraṅkaḷukkiṭaiyil naṉku vaḷaravillai.

ka.-6

⁠maṉṉikkavum eṅkō ceṉṟuviṭṭēṉ. ūrtōṟum -teru tōṟum antantak kirāmattil kaṟṟavarkaḷaik koṇṭu-avarkaḷukkuc ciṟitaḷavu ūkkattokai koṭuttu piḷḷaikaḷukkuk kalvi tarac coṉṉāl payaṉ viḷaiyum eṉa nampukiṟēṉ. nāṉ eṉ ciṟuvayatil-ūr periyatāka iruntālum, antak kālattil 1920-kku muṉ aracāṅkap paḷḷi illāta kāraṇattāl taṉippaṭṭa or āciriyariṭam tāṉ payiṉṟēṉ. piṉ mūṉṟāvatu vakuppu vantapōtu paḷḷikkūṭam vantatu. atuvum kānti aṭikaḷ kūṟiyatu pōṉṟu, eḷiya olaivēynta kūraik kaṭṭaṭam-maṇcuvar -maraccaṉṉaltāṉ. eṉiṉum aṉṟu āciriyarkaḷ naṉku piḷḷaikaḷukkuk kalvi maṭṭumiṉṟi, kalai, camūkaviyal pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum kaṟṟuttantaṉar. eṉ ūril aintām vakuppu payiṉṟapōtu alli arccuṉā eṉṟa nāṭakattil nāṉ kiruṣṇaṉāka naṭittatu maṭṭumiṉṟi, aṉṟu pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷum appaṭiyē iṉṟum niṉaivil uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē ūrtoṟum paḷḷi amaippatil-atilum kuṟainta celavil amaippatil atika kaṭiṉam illai. paḻaṅkālattil itait tiṇṇaip paḷḷikkūṭam eṉpārkaḷ. ām! appaḷḷikaḷil avvūrp piḷḷaikaḷ aṉaivarumē aintām vakuppu varaiyil naṉku paṭippārkaḷ. piṉ cilar mēl vakuppil cērap pērūrkaḷukkum nakaraṅkaḷukkum cella, cilar, ūril peṟṟōruṭaṉ toḻil ceyvar. eppaṭiyum aintām vakuppu varai aṉaivarum-perumpālum 100-kku 80 pēravatu paṭittiruppar. vēlaikkup pōkum piḷḷaikaḷukkum nāṉ muṉṉarē cuṭṭiyapaṭi viṭiyaṟkālai 5maṇikku muṟaiyam collum vakaiyil ātticūṭi toṭaṅki catakaṅkaḷ varaiyilum vāypāṭukaḷil kīḻvāy ilakkam varaiyilum kaṟṟukkoṭuppārkaḷ. ataṉāltāṉ anta nāḷil eṅkō ōriru iṭṅkaḷ tavirttu, ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum ūril oṟṟumaiyum enta vakaiyilum vēṟupāṭu kāṇa muṭiyāta orumaippāṭum nilaviya taṉmaiyiṉaik kāṇa muṭintatu. varalāṟṟu. aṭippaṭaiyil neṭuṅkālattukku

muṉ ceṉṟālum-caṅka kālattilō pallavar kālattilō ittakaiya ūr oṟṟumaiyiṉaik kāṇamuṭikiṟatu. piṉṉāl yār yārō iṅkē vara, piṟkālac cōḻarkālattuc cātip pirivukaḷ-camayap pērrāṭṭaṅkaḷ pala tōṉṟiṉa. iṉṟō pāratitācaṉ kūṟiyappaṭi vāḻkiṉṟār muppattu mukkōṭi makkaḷ' eṉṟāl-cūḻkiṉṟa pētam antat tokai irukkum. inta avala nilaiyai iṉṟaiya aracāṅkam eppaṭiyum pōkkittāṉ ākavēṇṭum.

pala nāṭukaḷaic cuṟṟivanta nāṉ intak kalvi muṟaiyiṉaip pōṟṟum amerikka nāṭṭiṉaik kaṇṭu viyantēṉ. mēlē nām kaṇṭa vakaiyil iṉṟēṉum ūriṉ ovvoru pakutiyum-vāṣiṅṭaṉ, cikākō uṭpaṭṭa periya nakaraṅkaḷilum antanta ellaiyil vāḻkiṉṟavarkaḷē ellāvaṟṟaiyum kavaṉittuk koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. teru amaippu, cuṟṟuppuṟat tūymai, kalvi, nalattuṟai ākiyavaṟṟai avarkaḷē oru kuḻu amaittu naṉku pārāmarittuk koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. antanta ellaiyil amainta vīṭṭuvari mutaliya viṣayaṅkaḷaiyum avarkaḷē vacūlittuk koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. inta muṟaiyiṉāl aṅkaṅkē kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷ takka vakaiyil ceyalpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itupaṟṟi eṉṉuṭaiya nūlil (ēḻu - nāṭukaḷil eḻupatu nāṭkaḷ) kuṟittuḷḷēṉ. inta nilai, tamiḻnāṭṭil irunta paḻaiya vaṭṭa, māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷai eṉakku niṉaivūṭṭiṉa. oru ciṟu ellaikku or uṟuppiṉar amaiya-cumār 20 allatu 40 ūrkaḷukku -20 allatu 30 kilō mīṭṭar. viṭṭattil amainta ūrkaḷukku avar aṭikkaṭi ceṉṟu, āvaṉa kāṇa, ūrmakkaḷum avaruṭaṉ kūṭit tamakku vēṇṭiya nalaṉkaḷaip peṟṟaṉar. piṉ pañcāyattu āṭci eṉṟa peyaril pala māṟṟaṅkaḷ.uṇṭāṉapōtilum pōtiya vaḷarcci illai. itupaṟṟi muṉṉarē viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉātaliṉ iṅkē atikam eḻutattēvai illai.

⁠iṉi ārampap paḷḷikaḷil amaiyum pāṭaṅkaḷaip paṟṟic ciṟitu kāṇpōm. taṟpōtu meṭrikulēṣaṉ pōṉṟa paḷḷikaḷil payilum māṇavarkaḷukku mika atika maṉa cumaikaḷ ēṟṟappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. aracāṅkap paḷḷikaḷilum pāṭattiṭṭam tēvaiyāṉa aḷavukku atikamākavē uḷḷatu. nāṉ itupaṟṟi muṉṉarum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷēṉ. mutal iraṇṭu vakuppukaḷil tāymoḻiyil eḻuttu, patam, irucol, muccol vākkiyaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟai moḻiyilum, eṇkaḷ oruvaricai-iruvaricai kūṭṭal, kaḻittal, ciṟu aḷavu perukkal pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum kaṟṟut tarutal vēṇṭum. ivaṟṟuṭaṉ antanta ūrkaḷukku ēṟpa, cuṟṟup puṟac cūḻalukku amaiya, ciṟu varalāṟukaḷ, nila amaippu muṟai, aṅkuḷḷa toḻil mukkiya talaivarkaḷ vāḻnta nilai, piṟavaṟṟaic collit tarutal vēṇṭum. ātticūṭi pōṉṟa ciṟu ciṟu . toṭarkaḷālāṉa aṟaneṟi paṟṟiya vākkiyaṅkaḷ payiṟṟappeṟal vēṇṭum. viḷaiyāṭṭu vakaiyilē palavaṟṟaic collittaralām. paḷḷinēram kālai 8 maṇi piṟpakal 2 maṇi eṉṟa aḷavil 30 māṇavar kaḷukku (orē vakuppu) or āciriyar aḷavil amaintu ceyalpaṭavēṇṭum, āciriyar kuḻantaikaḷōṭu kuḻantaiyāki viṭavēṇṭum. āciriyar eṉṟa accam eḻāta vakaiyil avrkaḷ naṭantukoḷḷavēṇṭum. vīṭṭil iruppataik kāṭṭilum paḷḷiyil iruppatē makiḻccikkuriyateṉa anta iḷam piñcu uḷḷaṅkaḷ niṉaikkavēṇṭum. paḷḷikkūṭam ētō caṭṭatiṭṭam, aṭi utai eṉṟa eṇṇattai avarkaḷiṭam uṇṭākkakkūṭātu. pala āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ naṭanta oru nikaḻcci eṉakku niṉaivukku varukiṟatu.

1958il nāṉ mturaip palkalaikkaḻakam toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉṉōṭiyāka, aṅkē tiyākarājā kallūriyil tamiḻ mutukalai vakupput toṭaṅka, aṉṟaiya tuṇai vēntar ē. el. mutaliyārāl aṉuppappeṟṟēṉ. oru muṟai nāṉ irayilil ceṉṟapōtu atē peṭṭiyil pampā

yiliruntu maturaikku (taṉ tāy vīṭṭiṟku) vantu koṇṭirunta oru tāyum 7.8 vayatu matikkattakka peṇṇum uṭaṉ payaṇam ceytaṉar. ciṟitu nēram pēcik koṇṭiruntapōtu nāṉ aṅkuḷḷa kallūriyil āciriyarākap paṇiyāṟṟuvataic coṉṉēṉ. ak kuḻantaiyuṭaṉum cirittup pēciṉēṉ. āyiṉum aṉṉaiyār eṉṉai taṉ makaḷukku 'ivar oru vāttiyār' eṉa aṟimukappaṭuttiya uṭaṉ ak kuḻantaiyiṉ mukam māṟupaṭṭatu. eṉakkup puriyavillai. aṉṉaiyār eṉṉaveṉṟu kēṭṭārkaḷ. antak kuḻantai 'vāttiyār cirippārō, nī poy collukiṟāy” eṉṟu patil colliṟṟu. piṟaku avarkaḷ pampāy mātuṅkāvil antak kuḻantai paṭikkum paḷḷiyil ataṉ vakuppil uḷḷa āciriyar ciṭu ciṭuppāka iruntu kuḻantaikaḷai eppōtum aṭittum vaitum pāṭam collik koṭuppār eṉṟārkaḷ. nāṉ uṇmaiyil varuntiṉēṉ. itu paṟṟi eṉ 'vaiyait tamiḻ' eṉṟa mutal nūlil oru kaṭṭuraiyē eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ, iḷam piñcu uḷḷattil āciriyaraip paṟṟi attakaiya eṇṇam uṇṭāṉāl-payam ēṟpaṭṭāl-atu eppaṭip paṭikka muṭiyum? eṉavē āciriyarkaḷ -ciṟappāka ārampappaḷḷi āciriyarkaḷ aṉpu kalanta tayuḷattōṭu iṉmukattōṭu kuḻantaikaḷukkup pāṭam kaṟpi kavēṇṭum. iṉṟu ceṉṉaiyil pala taṉiyār paḷḷikaḷil aintām vakuppu vakaiyil peṇkaḷē āciriyarāka uḷḷaṉar. tamiḻaka aracum iṉi ārampappaḷḷikaḷil peṇkaḷaiyē āciriyarkaḷāka niyamikkap pōvatākak kūṟiyatu āṟutal aḷikkiṉṟatu. peṇkaḷ tāyuḷḷam koṇṭavarkaḷātaliṉ avartam paṇpum paḻakkamum kuḻantaikaḷukku ātaravāka amaiyum eṉṟa aṭippaṭaiyilē ittakaiya ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceyyappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.

mutal iraṇṭām vakuppukaḷil inta vakaiyil ārampak kalvi avaravar vāḻum ūr, cuṟṟuc cūḻal, toṭakkappāṭam, pāṭṭu, kaṇakku eṉa amaiya, mūṉṟām .

vakuppu muṭiya muṟaiyāṉa pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷ amaiyalām. varalāṟu, nilanūl, kaṇakku, aṟiviyal tuḷikaḷ, nalvāḻvu, moḻikaḷ iraṇṭu (tāymoḻiyum maṟṟoṉṟum) eṉap pakuttu avaṟṟiṟkeṉap pāṭanūlkaḷ amaikkalām. mutal iraṇṭu vakuppukaḷukkum nāṉ mēlē kāṭṭiyapaṭi pāṭalnūlkaḷ ārampattil taṭaiyillai. āyiṉum kaṇitam. moḻi iraṇṭiṉait tavira, maṟṟavaṟṟiṟkup pāṭanūlkaḷ amaiyavaḻiyillai; avai perumpālum antantac cūḻalai oṭṭi amaivatāl mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintām vakuppukaḷukku ēṟṟavakaiyil pāṭaṅkaḷai uruvākka, takkavar amainta kuḻu amaittu, kuḻantaikaḷ uḷaṅkoḷum vakaiyil moḻippāṭaṅkaḷum piṟavum eḻutac collavēṇṭum paḻaṅ kālattil iraṇṭām vakuppuvarai ūr, ataṉ pakkac cūḻalai aṟinta kuḻantai, mūṉṟām vakuppil anta vaṭṭattaic cērnta nilanūl payiṟci peṟum. appaṭiyē varalāṟṟil cilavaṟṟai-irācarācaṉ, civāji, kāntiaṭikḷ pōṉṟavar varalāṟu mutaliyaṉa, ciṟuciṟu kataikaḷāka. piḷḷaikaḷ virumpum vakaiyil iṭampeṟac ceyyalām. maṟṟaiya kaṇakku iru moḻikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṟku vakuppukku ēṟṟa pāṭaṅkaḷ amainta nūlkaḷ acciṭalām. nāṉkām vakuppil māvaṭṭam, aintām vakuppil mānilam eṉṟa aḷavil pāṭaṅkaḷum moḻikaḷil, vakuppiṉ vaḷarccikku ēṟṟa cila ciṟu ilakkiyaṅkaḷum iṭam peṟalām. kaṇakku muṟai paṭippaṭiyāka vakuppukku ēṟpa vaḷarcci peṟumvakaiyil pāṭattiṭṭam amaiyalām. ivvāṟu ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷum muṟaiyāka amaiya, nallāciriyarkaḷ muṟaippaṭi naṭattiṉāl aintām vakuppuvaraiyil payilum oru māṇavaṉ kalviyiṉ teḷinta aṟivu peṟuvāṉ eṉpatu uṟuti.

nāṉ muṉṉamē kuṟittapaṭi paḷḷikkūṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷaippaṟṟic cintikka vēṇṭum. tiṇṇaip paḷḷik

kūṭaṅkaḷ iṉṟu naṭatta muṭiyātu. eṉiṉum kaṭṭaṭam illāta paḷḷikaḷē illai eṉṉumāṟu ceyya vēṇṭum, antanta ūrmakkaḷiṭamē itaiviṭṭāl avarkaḷ eḷitil ovvōr ūrilum paḷḷi amaittuviṭuvar. tamiḻnāṭṭil muppatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu irunta nilaiyil, kirāmaṅkaḷil paḷḷikaḷ amaikka ūkkamuṭaṉ, ūril uḷḷavarkaṭkum paṇivaḻiyō cāmāṉkaḷ (maram-kal mutaliyaṉa) vaḻiyō pāti tara, aracāṅkam pātitarum vakaiyil ceyalpaṭiṉ ellā ūrkaḷum paḷḷikaḷaip peṟum. muṉpu tamiḻ nāṭṭil inta vakaiyil ettaṉaiyō ūrkaḷil cālaikaḷ amaittaṉar. ērikaḷait tuppuṟavu ceytaṉar. kālvāykaḷ veṭṭiṉar. paḷḷikaḷum kaṭṭiṉar. iṉṟu palakōṭi celavu ceyvatākak kūṟum aracāṅkam, ūrtōṟum takkavakaiyil inta muṟaiyiṉai ēṟpāṭu ceyyiṉ payaṉ viḷaiyum eṉa nampukiṟēṉ. āṉāl avvāṟu tarum tokai yār yār kaiyilō ceṉṟu cērāmal ataṟkeṉavē celaviṭap peṟukiṟatā eṉpatai aracāṅkam eccarikkaiyākap pārttuk koḷḷ vēṇṭum. eṉavē ellāvakaiyilum ārampak kalvi-pattām vakuppu varai (ellāk kuḻantai kaḷukkum 5-14) amaiya aracu ēṟpāṭu ceyyiṉ, nāṭṭiṉ piṟa vaḷaṅkaḷ yāvum tāmākavē amaiyum.

iṉṟu pala taṉiyār niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum māṇavar kūṭṭaṅkaḷum eḻuttaṟiyā nilaiyiṉaip pōkka, oyvu nērattil utavuvatāka māvaṭṭam tōṟum aṟikkai varukiṉṟatu. iḷaiñarukku maṭṭumaṉṟi vayatu vantōrukkum kalvi kaṟpikkum iyakkam nāṭṭil valuppeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum ataṟkeṉa vakukkum vaḻimuṟaikaḷ kāraṇamākavō, vēṟu etaṉ aṭippaṭaiyilō anta muṟai naṉku naṭaipeṟavillai eṉap palarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. camūkacēvai ceyvatākak kūṟikkoṇṭu, paṭṭuṭuttu, paṭam eṭuttuk koṇṭu tam camūka cēvaiyaik kāṭṭuvōrum uṇṭu. uṇmaiyil tammaimaṟantu camūkacēvaiyil pāṭupaṭuvōrum

uṇṭu vēṇṭiyavar-vēṇṭātavar eṉṟu kāṇātu uṇmaiyil toṇṭu ceypavarkaḷai ūkkuvittu aracāṅkam kalviyai vaḷarkka vēṇṭum ūrtōṟum mutiyōr kallikkāka ārampap paḷḷiyiṉai ottu, eḻuttum eṇṇum collikkoṭuttu vayatuvantōrai ōraḷavāvatu kaṟṟavarākka vēṇṭum eṉa mattiya, mānila aracukaḷ kōṭikkaṇakkil paṇam celavu ceytum accelavukku ēṟṟa payaṉ viḷaiyavillai eṉa aracāṅkattārē collukiṉṟaṉar. ellāvaṟṟiṟkum kaiyūṭṭu illāta tiṭṭamāṉa mēṟpārvai muṟai amaiyiṉ kalvimaṭṭumaṉṟi, nāṭṭil ellāttuṟaikaḷum naṉku amaiyum. anta ākkaneṟikku iṉṟu nāṭāḷum nallavarkḷ vaḻivakuttu muyalavēṇṭum eṉakkēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ. -

aṇmaiyil tilliyil naṭanta kāmaṉvelt mānāṭṭil (25-9-91-28-9.91) nāṭukaḷil ārampap paḷḷiyil kalviyiṉ taram mikak kīḻnōkkic ceṉṟuḷḷatu eṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu. (The Hindu 29.9.91 P. 20) intiyāvum oru kāmaṉvelt nāṭutāṉē! paḷḷiyil cērum māṇavar eṇṇikkai vaḷarntuviṭṭatu eṉṟālum kaṭanta irupattaintu āṇṭukaḷil taram mikat tāḻntuḷḷataik kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. itu piṟa mēl paṭippukaḷaiyum pātittuḷḷataiyum cuṭṭikkāṭṭa avarkaḷ tavaṟavillai. 10kku 8 pēr ārampap paḷḷiyil cērntālum avaruḷ pātipēr uyarnilaippaḷḷiyai eṭṭip pārppatillai eṉpataic cuṭṭi, camutāya vaḷarcci aṅkē taṭaippaṭuvataiyum niṉaivu paṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. -

ārampap paḷḷikaḷiṉ cīrkēṭukaḷai - iṭamiṉmai, kaṭṭaṭam iṉmai, pāṭanūlkaḷ iṉmai, takuti vāynta āciriyar iṉmai pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum avarkaḷ kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. makkaḷ tokaip perukkattiṉāl paḷḷikaḷ vaḷarcci aṭaiya, atai niṟaivēṟṟa niti illānilai uṭaiya nāṭukaḷum eṇṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. inta

nilaiyil cil nāṭukaḷ mēnilaik kalviyiṉai-palkalaik kaḻakak kalviyiṉai-oru cilarukkē utavum kalviyiṉai vaḷarkkak kōṭi kōṭiyāka mēlum mēlum, celaviṭuvataiyum cintai ceytuḷḷaṉar. ulakavaṅki utava vanta nilaiyilum cila nāṭukaḷ ēṟkātu. eṅkō piṉṉaṭainta nilaiyil uḷḷatum eṇṇappaṭṭatu vaḷamāṉ kāmaṉ velt nāṭukaḷ ēḻai nāṭukaḷukku niti utava vēṇṭiya nilaiyiṉaiyum avarkaḷ cuṭṭik kāṭṭat tavaṟavillai. kāmaṉvelt uyarkalvi vaḷarccit tiṭṭamum (Commen Wealth Higher Education Support Scheme) ārampik kalvit tiṭṭamum cērntu vaḷarum nāṭukaḷil kalvi vaḷarkka vāyppaḷikkum nilaiyiṉaiyum eṇṇiyuḷḷaṉar.

nam pāratamum itai eṇṇa vēṇṭum. ārampakkalvi tiruntiṉāltāṉ piṟa aṉaittu maṭṭattu uyarkalvikaḷum uyarcciyuṟum. inta nilai iṉṟēl eṉṉākum eṉpataiyum eṇṇip pārkkavēṇṭum. āḷum nalla amaiccarkaḷ āḻntu eṇṇi āvaṉa kāṇpārkaḷāka.

intat talaippu accākik koṇṭirukkaiyil tiṉamaṇiyil (17-11-91-pak. 4) tiru. ta. paracurāmaṉ eṉpavar eḻutiya ‘toṭakkappaḷḷikaḷil taṭumāṟum tamiḻ’ eṉṟa kaṭṭurai veḷivantataik kaṇṭēṉ. atil avarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻmoḻi pōṟṟappeṟāta avala nilaiyiṉai naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷaṉar. toṭakka nilaiyil moḻikkē mukkiyattuvam taravēṇṭum’ eṉac cuṭṭi, moḻippāṭam ārampap paḷḷikaḷil peṟṟuḷḷa iḻinilaiyiṉaic cuṭṭikkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉait tavirttu intiyāvil vēṟu enta mānilattilum ulakil vēṟu enta nāṭṭilum ivvāṟu tāymoḻi-nāṭṭumoḻi tāḻttappeṟum avalanilai kiṭaiyātu itil vēṟu ‘nammai nāmē āḷukiṟōm’ eṉṟu nām perumaiyākap pēcik koḷḷukiṟōm. iṉiyākilum tamiḻnāṭṭil viḻippuṇarcci arumpumā? tamiḻaka aracu takkaṉa ceyyumā? poṟuttiruntu kāṇavēṇṭum.





﻿maḻalaiyar kalvi



* * *



iḷaṅkuḻantaikaḷ kalviyum pōṟṟappaṭa vēṇṭiya oṉṟu. 5 vayatil mutalvakuppil cērvataṟku muṉ ōriru vakuppukaḷait taṟpōtu palar teruvutōṟum naṭatti varukiṉṟaṉar. ‘L.K.G.’, ‘U.K.G.’ eṉṟa peyaril iḷaṅkuḻantaikaḷ vakuppiṉai iraṇṭākap pakuttu, paṭippiṉait tarukiṉṟaṉar. 3 vayatukku muṉpum paṇiyiṭai uḻalum makaḷir taṅkaḷ kuḻantaikaḷaik kāppakaṅkaḷil (Creches) viṭṭuc cellukiṉṟaṉar. inta iḷaṅkuḻantaikaḷukkup paṭippuṭaṉ, tēvaiyāṉa ūṭṭaccattuṭaṉ kūṭiya uṇavum tēvaippaṭukiṟatu. paḷḷikaḷil cattuṇavuk kūṭaṅkaḷ amaittu aracāṅkam utavi purivataip pōṉṟu. inta maḻalaikaḷ paḷḷikaḷaiyum naṉku parāmarikka vēṇṭum. iḷam maḻalaikaḷaip pātukākkum illaṅkaḷ avvaḷavākap payiṟṟu nilaiyaṅkaḷāka amaivatillai. perumpālum paṇimēṟcellum tāyar piḷḷaikaḷaip pātukāppāka viṭṭuc cellum iṭaṅkaḷākavē avai uḷḷaṉa. kuḻantaikaḷ aḻāmalum vāṭāmalum pārttukkoḷvatum vēṇṭumpōtu pāl, ciṟṟuṇavu pōṉṟavai aḷippatum tūṅkumpōtu tāyaippōla pakkattil iruntu uṟaṅka vaippatumtām avai ceykiṉṟaṉa. eṅkō ōriru iṭaṅkaḷil pāṭṭukaḷ kaṟṟut taruvatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ciṟuciṟu aṭikaḷil amainta pāṭalkaḷai mūṉṟu vayatu nirampāta maḻalaikaḷ vāymoḻiyākak kēṭka iṉimaiyāka irukkumallavā! āṉāl iṉṟu appāṭalkaḷ ellām āṅkilap pāṭalkaḷākavē uḷḷṉa. appiñcu uḷḷaṅkaḷil tāymoḻi uṇarvum paṟṟum aṟṟuppōka inta muṟai vaḻiceykiṉṟatu. orucila tavirttup perupālāṉavai veṟum pāl uṇpikkum-tūṅkavaikkum nilaiyaṅkaḷākavē uḷḷaṉa. eṉiṉum teruvutōṟum ittakaiya kāppakaṅkaḷ vaḷarntu varuvatāl, avaṟṟai muṟaippaṭutti, urimam vaḻaṅka vakaikaṇṭu, tāymoḻiyilēyē akkuḻattaikaḷaip paḻakka vaḻivakai kāṇavēṇṭum. amerikkāvilō iṅkilāntilō āṅkila moḻi uṇarvu maḻalai uḷḷaṅkaḷil niḻaliṭukiṉṟaṉa. pirāṉcil pirañcu moḻi piḷḷai uḷḷaṅkaḷaik koḷḷai koḷḷukiṉṟatu. ēṉ nam vaṭanāṭṭilum avaravar tāymoḻiyē vāymoḻiyāka iḷam piḷḷaikaḷ payila vaḻi ceykiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭiltāṉ āṅkila mōkam talaivirittāṭukiṟatu. ‘centamiḻnāṭeṉum pōtiṉilē iṉpat tēṉ vantu pāyutu kātiṉilē’, eṉṟu pārati kaṇṭa kaṉavu veṟum pāṭṭiṉilō aṉṟi varuṅkāla varalāṟṟilō irukkavē inta maḻalaiyar paḷḷikaḷ vaḻivakukkiṉṟaṉa. aracu aṉṟi vēṟu yārē itait taṭuttu niṟutta vallār!

mūṉṟu vayatu muṭintatum muṟaiyāṉa vakaiyil toṭakka vakuppil (L.K.G.) piḷḷaikaḷ cērkkap peṟukiṉṟaṉar. iḷam piḷḷaikaḷ (0-6 vayatil) nam makkaḷ tokaiyil nūṟṟukkup patiṉēḻu eṉṟa vakaiyil uḷḷaṉar eṉa aracāṅkak kaṇakku eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷatu (Page 114 Towards an Enlightened and Humane Society) avaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉa vaṟumai nilaiyil vāṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkuḻantaikaḷ 100kku 10 catavītamē mēlē kaṇṭavakaiyil maḻalaiyar kāppakattilum maḻalaiyar paḷḷikaḷilum iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉar. perumpālāṉa piḷḷaikaḷ vaḻivakai iṉṟi vāṭukiṉṟṉar. anta 100kku 10il perumpālāṉavar nakaraṅkaḷil uḷḷavarkaḷē. inta nilaiyil iḷam piḷḷaikaḷ vaḷarntāl kalviyilum patavikaḷilum ettaṉai maṇṭala kamiṣaṉ vantālum 100kku 27 allatu 37eṉa otukkiṉālum eṉṉa payaṉ? inta maḻalaiyar paḷḷikaḷil peruntokai celaviṭṭuc cērntu payilum piḷḷai kaḷōṭu maṟṟaiya 90 catavikitam piḷḷaikaḷ evvāṟu pōṭṭiyiṭa muṭiyum? appaṭiyē mutaṟ vakuppil cērntālum iṭaiyil 2, 3, 4, 5 il varukiṉṟa piḷḷaikaḷ ettuṇaiyar? kirāmaṅkaḷtōṟum - nakaril kuṭicaip pakutikaḷ tōṟum inta avalanilaiyil uḷḷa kuḻantaikaḷaik kāṇamuṭikiṉṟatē! ataṟku oru vaḻivakai kāṇa vēṇṭāmā! 14 vayatukku uṭpaṭṭa ellāk kuḻantaikaḷukkum kaṭṭāyak kalvi taravēṇṭum eṉṟa araciyal cācaṉa 45vatu pakutippaṭi inta iḷaṅkuḻantaikaḷ aṉaittum orē vakaiyāṉa kalviyiṉaik kaṟka vaḻikāṇa vēṇṭāmā? irāmamūrttik kuḻu itai ārāynta vakaiyil itaṟku vimōcaṉam kaṇṭatākat tām kāṇavillai eṉṟum makaḷir, kuḻantai vaḷarccit tuṟaikkum kalvit tuṟaikkum ōr iṇaippu irukkavēṇṭumeṉṟum ataṉ vaḻiyē iḷam kuḻantaikaḷ uḷḷam kaṟpaṉa kaṟṟu, tāymoḻip paṟṟum peṟa vaḻi uṇṭu eṉṟum cuṭṭik kāṭṭi, āṉāl atu ceyalmuṟaiyil illai eṉpataiyum kuṟikkiṟatu (pak. 115). ataṉ parinturaiyil (116) makaḷir, piḷḷaikaḷ vaḷarccittuṟai, iḷaṅkuḻantaikaḷ kalviyil paṅkukoḷḷavēṇṭum eṉpatai valiyuṟuttik kūṟiyuḷḷatu. kalvittuṟaiyum, araciyal cācaṉa 45vatu vitippaṭi, ikkuḻantaikaḷ kalviyaik kaṇṇeṉak kākka vēṇṭiya kaṭamaiyiṉaiyum cuṭṭikkāṭṭi uḷḷatu. mattiya aracum mānila aracukaḷum inta vakaiyil ottuḻaittuc ceyalpaṭ vēṇṭum eṉavum cuṭṭiyuḷḷatu, iḷam kuḻantaikaḷ tāyaṉpukku ēṅkum aḷaviṟku varakkūṭātu eṉpataiyum eṇṇi, tāyiṉaiyē aṟiyāta nilaiyil eppaṭi ataṉ vāḻvu amaiyum eṉpataiyum karutip pārkkavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. maḻalaiyar pātukāppu illaṅkaḷil viṭappeṟum kuḻantaikaḷ yār kaiyilō iruntu, eppaṭiyō uṇṭu, uṟaṅkip poḻutaik kaḻikka, mālaiyil aṉṉaiyar variṉum avarkaḷ vēlaikaḷukku iṭaiyil avartam pācattaiyum pariviṉaiyum peṟāmal varukiṉṟaṉa. piṉ, ceṉṟa talaimuṟaikkum intat talaimuṟaikkum perutta māṟupāṭu uṇṭākiṟatē eṉṟu varuntuvatāl eṉṉa payaṉ? aṇmaiyil tolaikkāṭciyil ittakaiya oru katai (24.9.91) kāṭṭappeṟṟu conta orē makaḷ vēṟoruvaṉōṭu ceṉṟu maṟaintataiyum poruṭpaṭuttātu, vāṇipa vēlaiyilē tantaiyum uyar camutāyac cūḻalilē tāyum ceṉṟataik kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu. inta nilaiyil tāyppācamē akaṉṟuviṭa, antap peṇ iṟutiyil tāy tantaiyaik kaṇṭapōtum kaḷippeytātu putiya maṉitaraik kāṇpatu pōlavē niṉṟāḷ. itu perumpālum perum celvar vīṭukaḷilum tāy tantaiyar iruvarum paṇipuriyum vīṭukaḷilum naṭaipeṟum nikaḻcci tāṉē. tāyaṉpē ivvāṟu illaiyākumpōtu tāy moḻippaṟṟum tāynāṭṭup paṟṟum eppaṭi amaiyum? inta nilai nāṭṭil vaḷariṉ ‘intiya nāṭṭiṉ teṉ kōṭiyil tamiḻ eṉṟa oru moḻi vaḻakkil iruntatu’ eṉa varalāṟṟil paṭikkum vakaiyilum tāynāṭu pala vakaiyil citaṟuṇṭu pōkum vaḻiyilum varuṅkālam amaiyumē eṉṟu yārum kavalaippaṭṭatākat teriyavillai. inta avalanilaiyai nīkkiṉālaṉṟi nāṭu uyyumāṟu illai eṉpatu uṟuti.

ikkuṟai nīkka irāmamūrttik kuḻu cila vitikaḷaik kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu. (pak. 119) āṉāl aracāṅkam avaṟṟaip piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭumē. ettaṉaiyō kuḻukkaḷ mattiya mānila aracāṅkaṅkaḷ viṭutalai peṟṟapiṉ nāḷtōṟum niyamikkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ parinturaikaḷai appaṭiyē ēṟṟucceyalpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉavā? illaiyē pērukkuk kuḻu amaittu ūrukku upatēcittuc ceyalil piṉṉaṭaiyum nilai nāṭṭil irukkum varaiyil nāṭu nāṭākavā irukkum. eṟumpukaḷ varicaiyāka oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka ūrntu nēriya vaḻiyil cellukiṉṟaṉa. paṟavaikaḷ mālaippoḻutil tam kūṭunōkki viraintu paṟakkiṉṟaṉa kōḻi viṭiyalil eḻuntu tavaṟātu kuraleḻuppi ‘veyyōṉai vāvuparittērēṟi vāveṉṟaḻaippaṉapōl’ kūvukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉ? ōraṟivuṭaiya maraṅkaḷum ceṭikaḷumkūṭak kālam tavaṟāmal paṇiyāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. māvum palāvum kōṭaiyil paḻuttup payaṉtarukiṉṟaṉa: malliyum mullaiyum iḷavēṉilil mukiḻttu maṇam vīcukiṉṟaṉa. avai aṉaittum iṉṉār iṉiyār eṉṟu pārppatillai; uyarntār tāḻntār eṉṟu nōkkuvatillai. eṉ kaṭcikkāraṉ, veṉṟavaṉ eṉṟu vēṟupaṭuttuvatillai. āṉāl āṟaṟivuṭaiyavaṉākiya maṉitaṉ etaik kālattil ceykiṉṟāṉ? ñālam karutiṉum kaikūṭum kālam karuti iṭattāl ceyiṉ eṉṟa kuṟaḷ ‘pas’cil-pēruntukaḷil eḻutuvataṟkuttāṉē oḻiya taṉ vāḻvukku illai eṉṟu tāṉē ceyalāṟṟukiṉṟāṉ. inta nilaiyil aṭuttu varum iḷam camutāyattai-maḻalaiyar maṉattai ippōtē nēriya vaḻiyil celuttāviṭṭāl nāṭu eṉṉākum? aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ illāta cātic caṇṭaikaḷum camayac caṇṭaikaḷum,(teṉ āppirikkāviṉ niṟaccaṇṭai uṭpaṭa) avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē eḻum kolaikaḷum iṉṟu nāḷtōṟum nam nāṭṭil naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉavē. makkaḷ kaṭattalum kaḷḷap poruḷkaḷ kaṭattalum iṉṟupōl eṉṟēṉum iruntatuṇṭā aracu maṭṭumiṉṟit taṉimaṉitaṉum itupaṟṟic cintikka vēṇṭāmā! camayat talaivarkaḷ-camūkat talaivarkaḷ eṇṇip pārkka vēṇṭāmā! eṇṇip pārttu iḷaṅ kuḻantaikaḷukku oṉṟiya neṟiyil, uṟṟa kalviyaiyum piṟa nalaṉkaḷaiyum tantu varuṅkāla camutāyattaiyāvatu vāḻavaippārkaḷā?

inta iḷam piḷḷaikaḷukku veṟum āṅkila mōkattai vaḷarkkātu, namnāṭṭuc cuṟṟuc cūḻal, paṇpāṭu, vāḻkkai ivaṟṟiṟku ēṟpa, pāṭalkaḷaiyum pāṭaṅkaḷaiyum kaṟṟuttara ēṟpāṭu ceyyavēṇṭum. tamiḻnāṭṭaip poṟuttavaraiyil ataṟkēṟpa ātticūṭi koṉṟai vēntaṉ toṭaṅki aṟaneṟikaḷai vaṟpuṟutti vaḻikāṭṭum pērilakkiyaṅkaḷ varicai varicaiyāka-muṟai muṟaiyāka vakuppu nilaikku ēṟpa eṇṇaṟṟu uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟaiyē vēṇṭām eṉṟu otukkum aḷavukku iṉṟu tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟiviṭṭāṉē! tiruppip pārttut tirunta vēṇṭum.

iṉṟu tantai tāyp pēṇ, ūruṭaṉ kūṭivāḻ, oppuravu oḻuku aṉṉaiyum pitāvum muṉṉaṟi teyvam, ālayam toḻuvatu cālavum naṉṟu. ivai pōṉṟaṉa tamiḻk kuḻantaikaḷ aṟiyātaṉa.

ittakaiya iḷaṅ kuḻantaikaḷ (0-6) payiṟṟuvataṟkut taṉit tiṟaṉ tēvai. uyarnta paṭṭaṅkaḷō, vēṇṭāta pāṭaṅkaḷ, moḻikaḷaip payiṟṟap payiṟcikaḷō tēvai illai. kuḻantaikaḷ payiṟcippaḷḷi (Nursery Training School) eṉa oru cila taṉiyār naṭattukiṉṟaṉa. avaiyum tēvaiyāṉa muṟaiyil-terinta vakaiyil ceyalpaṭuvatillai. pattām vakuppu payiṉṟapiṉ āciriyar payiṟci tāṉē (Sec Grade) iṉṟu aṭittaḷap payiṟciyāka uḷḷatu. atil payilpavarkaḷ oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu vakuppu varaiyil āciriyarāka irukkalām. avarkaḷum 1, 2 mutaliya vakuppukaḷukkuc collit tarum tiṟaṉ peṟuvakaiyil avarkaḷ payiṟci peṟuvatillai. payiṟcip paḷḷikaḷiṉ pāṭattiṭṭam mutalil māṟṟi amaikkappeṟal vēṇṭum.

iḷaṅ kuḻantaikaḷukkup pāṭam payiṟṟum payiṟcip paḷḷikaḷaip paṟṟi aracu kavaṉam celuttalām. peṇ iṉattukkup peritum utava niṉaikkum iṉṟaiya aracāṅkam aintām vakuppu varai makaḷiraiyē āciriyarāka niyamikkap pōvatākak kūṟiyuḷḷatu. nallatē. āṉāl kuḻantaikaḷ vakuppiṟku uriya āciriyarkaḷukkup payiṟci illai. itai eṇṇip pārkkavēṇṭum illaiyāṉāl aṭippaṭai illāta kaṭṭaṭam pōṉṟu uṭaṉē iṭintu viḻum. itē camutāya vāḻvāka nāṭṭuvāḻvu amaintu viṭalām. ippayiṟciyum avvammakkaḷ vāḻum cūḻalukku ēṟpa, anniya vāṭai vīcāta vakaiyil amaiyavēṇṭum. māvaṭṭattukku oru payiṟcip paḷḷiyāvatu uṭaṉ toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.

nāṉ mēlē cuṭṭiyapaṭi kuḻantaikaḷ tāṅkaḷ vāḻum camutāyattai oṭṭiyē vaḷarkkappeṟal vēṇṭum. itaṉāl ēḻai ēḻaiyākavē irukkavēṇṭumeṉṟō celvaṉ mēlum celvam peṟṟavaṉāka ākavēṇṭum eṉpatō karuttaṉṟu. puliyaip pārttup pūṉai cūṭu pōṭṭuk koṇṭatu eṉpataṟku ēṟpa yārō eppaṭiyō vāḻkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu ataṟkāka nam kuḻantaikaḷum anta vaḻiyil paṭikka vēṇṭuvatillai. ēḻai nāṭāka uḷḷa intiyāvil-perumpālum kirāmaṅkaḷē amainta intiyāvil akkirāmac cūḻnilaikkēṟpa ārampakalvi amaiyavēṇṭum. iḷaṅ kuḻantaikaḷ uḷḷattē taṉ ūr taṉ uṟaviṉar, uṟṟār, taṉṉaippōṉṟa makkaḷ, ūrpuṟac cūḻal pōṉṟa vakaiyil amaiyap pāṭaṅkaḷ collittara vēṇṭum. piṉ nāṉ ārampaḷḷi paṟṟiya eḻuttil kāṭṭiya paṭi vaṭṭam, māvaṭṭam, mānilam, piṟaku, ulakam eṉṟu mella mella, vaḷara vēṇṭum. iḷaṅ kuḻantaikaḷ uḷḷam-kaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟa uḷḷam! eṉavē taṉ tāy moḻi-tāy tantai uṟṟār uṟaviṉar paṟṟu, ūrppaṟṟu mutaliyaṉa amaiya, piṟapiṉ toṭara vaḻi kāṭṭiyākap palavakaiyil kuḻantaikaḷ kalvi (0-6) amaiya vēṇṭum. itu paṟṟi irāmamūrtti āyvukkuḻu teḷivāka iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷ (125-126) parinturai ceytuḷḷatu. avaṟṟai mattiya mānila aracukaḷ piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum ivvāṟu iḷaṅ kuḻantaikaḷ uḷḷattē nāṭṭuppaṟṟum moḻippaṟṟum vaḷarum vakaiyil kalvi muṟai amaippiṉ varum cantati vaḷamuṟṟu vāḻa vaḻi uṇṭākum. nāṭṭu makkaḷ tām koṇṭuḷḷa ellā vēṟṟumaikaḷaiyum piñcu uḷḷaṅkaḷil pukāmal pātukāttiṭa muyala vēṇṭum. ataṟku ēṟpa mattiya mānila aracukaḷ takka neṟippaṭum kuḻantaikaḷ-iḷaṅ kuḻantaikaḷukkup paḷḷikaḷai attuṟaiyil payiṟci peṟṟa nallāciriyarkaḷaik koṇṭu naṭatta ēṟpāṭu ceyyavēṇṭum. aintil vaḷaiyātatu aimpatil vaḷaiyātu eṉpatu tamiḻp paḻamoḻi. eṉavē aintu vayatu nirampiya piḷḷai uḷḷaṅkaḷil patiyum eṇṇamtāṉ mukkiyam eṉpatai uṇarntu avarkaḷai āṟṟuppaṭuttum nallāciriyar payiṟcip paḷḷikaḷai uṭaṉ toṭaṅki ōrāṇṭu allatu īrāṇṭu appayiṟciyai muṭikka vaḻikāṇa vēṇṭum. ūrtoṟum arcāṅkamē - ūrāṭciyāḷar vaḻiyō piṟavaḻiyō attakaiya nallāciriyar koṇṭa maḻalaip paḷḷikaḷai niṟuvi, avai naṉku naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉavā eṉpataik kavaṉikka vaḻikāṇa vēṇṭum inta nilaiyiṉai mutalil ceyyiṉ, varuṅkāla nāṭu kalviyāl maṭṭumaṉṟip piṟa ellā vakaiyālum ēṟṟamuṟum eṉpatu teḷivu.

mutal vakuppukku muṉ uḷḷa ‘L.K.G. U.K.G’ vakuppukaḷaip paṟṟic ciṟitu kāṇpōm. cīruṭai uṭuttu talaicīvi perum cumaiyākap puttakaṅkaḷait tāṅkikkoṇṭu iḷañ celvaṅkaḷ paḷḷikku varuvatum, avarkaḷai aḻaittu vantu viṭṭu, piṉ tirumpa aḻaittuc cella, tāyarum piṟarum varuvatum ovvoru paḷḷiyilum kaṇ koḷḷāk kāṭciyākattāṉ uḷḷatu. cila peṟṟōrkaḷ-iruvarum paṇiceypavarkaḷāka iruntu, vīṭṭil pārkka vēṟu yārum illā nilaiyil, mālai avarkaḷ tirumpum varaiyil, paḷḷiyilēyē taṅka vaittukkoḷḷat taṉi ēṟpāṭukaḷum cila iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. kuḻantaikaḷ maḻalaimoḻi kēṭṭu makiḻavēṇṭiya nallāciriyarkaḷ, cilacamayaṅkaḷil vallāciriyarkaḷāvatum uṇṭu. inta nilaiyiṉāl añcum kuḻantaikaḷukkuk kalviyilēyē veṟupput tōṉṟum cīruṭai.yum piṟavum kuḻantai uḷḷattil vēṟupāṭukaḷ tōṉṟa

ka.-7 vaḻiyillai. perumpālum ivvakuppukaḷ pātināṭkaḷō, cumār mūṉṟumaṇi nēramō naṭappatāl uṇavu vēṟu pāṭṭiṉaik kāṇavum muṭiyātu. cila kuḻantaikaḷukkup peṟṟōr iṭaivēḷaiyil ētēṉum parukak koṇṭu vantu taruvar. atu atika vēṟṟumaiyai ēṟpaṭuttātu. eṉiṉum cila peṟṟōr taṅkaḷ piḷḷaikaḷukkut taṉic calukai tarak kaṭṭāyappaṭuttum pōtutāṉ tollai uṇṭākiṟatu.

L.K,G. U.K G. vakuppukaḷil eḻuttu vēlaikaḷ atikam illai eṉṟālum kuḻantaikaḷ virumpiyapaṭi vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷ tīṭṭ, vaṇṇak kōlkaḷ paḷḷikaḷil tarappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ak kuḻantaikaḷ kiṟukki varaiyum paṭaṅkaḷai āciriyariṭamum peṟṟōriṭamum kāṭṭi avai makiḻum pōtum nām nammai maṟakkiṟōm. ciṟu ciṟu aṭikaḷā lākiya pāṭalkaḷaip pāṭumpōtum kēṭpōrukku makiḻcci piṟakkiṟatu. āṉāl avai vēṟṟumoḻiyilaṉṟō perumpālāṉa paḷḷikaḷil payiṟṟappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. inta iru vakuppilum tamiḻē illāta paḷḷikaḷ ceṉṉai nakaril pala uḷḷaṉa. itaṉait taṭukka vaḻikāṇavēṇṭum, iḷamaiyiṟ kalvi cilaiyil eḻuttu eṉṟa vākku eṉṟum uṇmaiyākumātalāl inta iḷamaiyil kaṟṟuttarum kalviyē varum kālac camutāyattai vāḻavaikkum-vaḷara vaikkum. inta uṇmaiyiṉai eṇṇi aracum piṟarum ceyalāṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉak kēṭṭukkoḷkiṟēṉ.

intiyā urimai peṟṟu nāṟapattaintu āṇṭukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. appoḻutē ellārukkum (3-15) vayatuc kalvi aṟivu taravēṇṭum eṉṟa muyaṟciyil nāṭu īṭupaṭṭu. iruntāl iṉṟu 50 vayatukku uṭpaṭṭavar aṉaivarum kaṟṟavarkaḷākavē iruppar. iṉṟu maruttuvap paṭṭam peṟupavar aracāṅkp paṇiyil cēra virumpiṉāl ōrāṇṭu kirāmaṅkaḷil paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa viti koṇṭu varap pōvatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. cātāraṇap paṭṭam peṟa virumpuvarkaḷukkum paṭippu muṭintapiṉ ōrāṇṭu kirāmattil paṇipurintāltāṉ paṭṭam peṟamuṭiyum eṉṟu ēṟpāṭu ceyyavēṇṭum eṉac cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. intak karuttiṉaittāṉ, nāṉ muṉṉarē kuṟittapaṭi, 1947il cutantiram peṟṟa uṭaṉē intiyā mutalcaṭṭamāka inta muṟaikku ēṟpa, oru caṭṭam iyaṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu ‘intiya mutaṟ caṭṭam’ eṉṟa nūlil eḻutiṉēṉ. atil paṭṭam peṟupavar ōr āṇṭil kirāmaṅkaḷil ceṉṟu, aṅkētaṅki, avarkaḷoṭu paḻaki, avartam ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷ aṟintu, tuyartuṭaittu, kalvi aḷittu, toḻilperuka vaḻikaṇṭu eṉṉeṉṉa ceyya vēṇṭum eṉa viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. nāṭakamaṇi T.K. caṇmukam ataip pārāṭṭit tām ataṉai nāṭakamāka naṭatta virumpuvatākavum kūṟiṉār. iraṇṭoru paḷḷikaḷ naṭittaṉa. annūlukku aṉṟaiya kalvi amaiccarāka irunta māṇpumiku tiru. paktavatcalam avarkaḷum pārāṭṭi muṉṉurai tantuḷḷārkaḷ. eṉiṉum inta muṟai ittaṉai āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittum iṉṟum piṉpaṟṟap peṟavillai. immuṟai aṉṟē vaḻakkattil, vantirukkumāyiṉ kirāmaṅkaḷil kalvi kaṟṟōr eṇṇikkai mika atikamāka vaḷarntirukkum. kallātār illai eṉṟa nilaiyē uruvāki irukkum. āṉāl iṉṟu anta nilai illai. iṉṟaiya nilaiyil eḻuttaṟiyār nilai paṟṟi aracāṅka veḷiyīṭṭiṉaiyē iṅkē tara niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ. nāṭṭiṉ avalanilaiyiṉaiyum, mēṟkoṇṭa muyaṟcikaḷaiyum. ceyya vēṇṭiyatāya kālap paṇiyiṉaiyum atu viḷakkuvatai ōraḷavu kāṇa iyalum. ulakil uḷḷa kallātār eṇṇikkaiyil pāti aḷavu nam nāṭṭiltāṉ irukkum nilaiyai eṇṇi ovvoru intiyaṉum varunti veṭkappaṭa vēṇṭāmā? eṅkō orucārār muṉṉēṟi viṭṭatākavum perumpālōr paṭittatākavum kūṟik koṇṭu, ēḻaikaḷ-kirāma makkaḷ-peṇkaḷ ivarkaḷuḷ perumpālōr paṭikkātirukkum nilaiyiṉai eṇṇip, pārppatillai. atilum iṉṟu nakaraṅkaḷil vaḷarum āṅkila mōkattiṉ āḻattil putaintu kiṭakkum mākkaḷ, tammaip pōl intiya makaṉāka-makaḷāka vāḻum eṇṇaṟṟa makkaḷai eṇṇip pārppatillai; māṟāka ēḷaṉam ceyvar. aracāṅka muyaṟcikaḷum avvaḷavākap payaṉ taruvatillai. anta aracāṅka aṟikkaiyiṉai iṅkē tarukiṉṟēṉ.

COMMITTEES PERSPECTIVE

7-2-1. The Resolution setting up the committee to review NPE, inter alia, states:

“Despite efforts at social and economic development since attainment of Independence a majority of our people continue to remain deprived or education, which is one of the basic needs for human development. It is also a matter of grave concern that our people comprise 50 per cent of the world's illiterate, and large sections of children have to go without acceptable level of primary education. Government accords the highest priority, to education-both as a human right and as the means for bringing about a transformation towards a more humane and enlightened society. There is need to make education an effective instrument for securing a status of equality for women, and persons belonging to the backward classes and minorities. Moreover, it is essential to give a work and employment orientation to education

POST POLICY IMPLEMENTATION

7-3-0 In pursuances of the Policy, a National Literacy Mission was established in 1988. In quantitative terms, the Mission seeks to impart functional literacy to 80 million illiterate persons in 15-35 age group: 80 million by 1990 and an additional 50 million by 1995. The emphasis of the Mission is not on mere numbers but on attainment of certain pre - determined norms and parameters of literacy, numeracy, functionality and awareness. Under this programme 2,84,000 centres are functioning in the country with an estimated involvement of 84 lakh adult learners (about 35 lakh men and 49 lakh women). Six lakh literacy kits have been delivered to students, volunteers. Over 30,000 Jan Shikshan Nilayams have been sanctioned to . provide post - Literacy programmes. Over 800 voluntary Agencies have also been involved in the process. A Mass Campaign under NLM was launched by the then Prime Minister in May, 1988. Similar campaigns were launched by 24 States and UTs on the same date and after. The Director General. NLM in his presentation before the . NPERC stated that the Programme has suffered due to shortage of funds. As against the barest minimum requirement of funds amounting to Rs. 189 crores during 1989-90, the actual amount provided was of the order of 76.17 crores. The position was somewhat improved in 1990-91 when a sum of Rs.96 crores was provided, considering the magnitude of the problem of illiteracy the large uncover area and the need for mobilisation (A total of Rs.227 crores was spent under NLM during the years 1987-88 to 1989-90). Much more funds are required even for implementing a purely volunteer-based, campaign-oriented plan of action than what has been provided. (Towards an Enlightened and Humane Society-Pages 198 & 194)

nam nāṭṭilēyē malaiyāḷa mānilattil nūṟṟukku nūṟu kaṟṟavarkaḷ irukkum pōtu ovvoru mānilamum attakaiya muyaṟcikaḷai ēṉ mēṟkoḷḷalākātu. , oru cila mānilaṅkaḷ ōraḷavu muyalkiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum pōtiya aḷavu illai eṉpataiyē nāṭṭunilai kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉaip poṟuttavaraiyil kōvai māvaṭṭattil ammāvaṭṭa āṭciyar talaimaiyil oru eḻuttaṟivippu iyakkam amaintateṉa aṟikiṟōm. (tiru. irāmamūrttiyiṉ veḷiyīṭu 196) 5.25 ilaṭcam eḻuttaṟiyā makkaḷukku utava, atai 21 pakutikaḷākap pirittu cumār 50,000 taṉiyār utavi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ vaḻiyē ceyalāṟṟi 1990 ēppiral mutal 1992 mārccu muṭiyumuṉ, ammāvaṭṭattaiyē muḻutum kaṟṟa māvaṭṭamākka muyalvatāka aracāṅkak kuṟippu kāṭṭukiṟatu. eṉiṉum aṅkēyum avarkaḷ niṉaittapaṭi payaṉviḷaiyavillai eṉṟē kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ām! kaṭanta nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷil iḷam piḷḷaikaḷ (5-14) kaṭṭāyamākap payila vēṇṭiya tiṭṭattiṉai eppāṭupaṭṭēṉum niṟaivēṟṟi iruntāl iṉṟu inta avalanilai irātu allavā! taṟpōtu tamiḻnāṭṭil puttuṇarcci arumpiyuḷḷatu. putukkōṭṭai māvaṭṭattiṉaip pūraṇakkalvi aṟi māvaṭṭamākka muṉaintuḷḷaṉar eṉṟa ceyti aṇmaiyil veḷivantatu. kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭam ēṟakkuṟaiya niṟaivu eytum nilaiyil uḷḷatu eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nāṭṭil pala niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ tāmē muṉvantu, inta eḻuttaṟivu iyakkattil paṅku koṇṭu pāṭupaṭa muṉaikiṉṟatu eṉak kēṭṭaṟikiṟōm. paḷḷiyilum kallūriyilum payilum māṇavarkaḷ tāmākavē muṉvantu ūrtōṟum ceṉṟu inta ākkappaṇikku āvaṉa ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṟōm. iṉṟaiya mānila aracum inta vakaiyil mattiya araciṉ tuṇaiyuṭaṉ pala kōṭi celaviṭṭu intaeḻuttaṟivu iyakkattiṉaic ceyalpaṭuttukiṉṟatu eṉpataik kēṭka makiḻcci piṟakkiṉṟatu. aracāṅkam pala āṇṭukaḷāka itaṟkeṉak kalvittuṟaiyil oru taṉippirivē amaittu, ataṟkeṉat taṉi iyakkunaraiyum irukka vaittu, ūrtōṟum ceyalpaṭuvakaiyil pala atikārikaḷai niyamittu āvaṉa ceytu varukiṉṟatu. ovvoru pañcāyattu ellaiyilum taṉi aluvalarkaḷ niyamikkap peṟṟu, avarkaḷ ūrtoṟum ceṉṟu mutiyōr kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷ amaittup pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. mutiyavarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya palakai, puttakam, karumpalakai, pōṉṟavaṟṟai iṉāmāka aḷittu aḻaikkiṉṟaṉar. mutalil oru cilar cērntālum mella mella. avaruḷ palar viṭṭu vilakukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa kuralē kēṭkiṟatu. ‘vantu paṭippataṟku eṉakkeṉṉa taruvāy’ eṉṟa kēḷviyum kēṭkap peṟukiṟatām! eṅkō oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil tāmākavē valiya vantu kaṟkum periyavarkaḷ-eḻuttaṟivaṟṟavar irukkiṟārkaḷ eṉak kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm. avarkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷum vakaiyil paṭaṅkaḷ, viḷakka nūlkaḷ, iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟaiyum aracāṅkam tarukiṟatu. avarkaḷukku eppoḻutu ōyvu kiṭaikkumō appōtu vakuppukaḷai naṭattavum tayārāka uḷḷatu. eṉiṉum makkḷ ārvam kāṭṭavillai eṉkiṉṟaṉar. iḷampiḷḷaikaḷ paṭikkum paḷḷikaḷilēyē anta nilai eṉa aṟikiṟōm pakal uṇavum pālum paḻamum piṟavum aḷikkiṉṟa kāraṇattāl tāṉ paḷḷiyil taṟpōtu atikamāṉa piḷḷaikaḷ payilkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu aracāṅkamē takaval tarukiṉṟatē. inta nilai ellām pōkka vaḻi illaiyā! maruntillaiyā aracāṅka muṉaippu pōtātā! paṇiyāḷar muyaṟci paṟṟākkuṟaiyil amaikiṉṟatā? teriyavillaiyē.

1981 makkaḷ kaṇakkuppaṭi nam nāṭṭil payilāta āṇkaḷ 53% ākavum peṇkaḷ 75% iruntaṉar. ippōtō inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilō nāṉ mēlē kāṭṭiyapaṭi, ulakak kallātār eṇṇikkaiyil pāti intiyāvil irukkum eṉkiṉṟaṉar. itaṟkuk kāraṇam makkaḷ itil pōtiya kavaṉam celuttāmaiyētāṉ. nāṭṭil oṉṟum kallātavaṉ-kaittoḻil viṉaiñaṉ oru nāḷaikku 50, 60 eṉṟum ataṟku mēlum campātikkiṟāṉ. peṇkaḷum appaṭiyē irupattaintu, muppatu eṉa nāḷtōṟum campātikkiṉṟaṉar. iḷampiḷḷaikaḷum kūṭa 15 vayatil-iṉṉum 10 vayatil kūṭa 10 allatu 15, 20 varai nāṭkūli peṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟaic cēmittu vāḻkkaiyiṉaic cemmaippaṭuttukiṟārkaḷā eṉpatu vēṟu. āṉāl ivvāṟu peṟum varuvāyiṉai viṭuttu, paṭikka varuvatu kaṭiṉam. ‘paṭittāl eṉṉa itaiviṭa atikamāka varap pōkiṟatā?’ eṉṟa kēḷvi avarkaḷ uḷḷattil eḻukiṉṟatu. mēlum palar mālai vēḷaikaḷil kuṭikkum pōtaip poruḷukkum aṭimaiyākiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ vīṭṭu vēlaikku uṭpaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iravilō aṉṟi viṭiyaṟ kālaiyilō camaiyal ceytu, uṇṭu. vēlaikkup pōkum pōtum matiya uṇavukkāka uṭaṉ eṭuttuc ceṉṟāltāṉē nāḷ muḻutum uḻaikka muṭiyum. inta nilaiyil avarkaḷ paṭippateṅkē? piḷḷaikaḷ varalām. avarkaḷai nēriya vaḻiyil āṟṟuppuṭutta vēṇṭāmā? aracāṅkam inta avalanilaiyaip pōkka ētētō vaḻivakaikaḷaik kaṇṭu koṇṭutāṉ varukiṟatu. āyiṉum avaṟṟaic ceyalpaṭuttuvatil uḷḷa kuṟaipāṭukaḷ kaḷaiyappeṟal vēṇṭum.

periyōrkaḷukku eṉa ōrāṇṭō īrāṇṭō kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ payila varuvōr kuṟaintatu pātināṭkaḷāvatu variṉ ōraḷavu eḻuttaṟivu peṟa muṭiyum. āṉāl ataṟkum pañcam eṉkiṉṟaṉar. ētō maṟṟavar vaṟpuṟuttalukkākac cērntatākavum vēlai veṭṭi aṟṟavar ceyal itu eṉṟum niṉaikkiṉṟavar iṉṟum palar uḷḷaṉar. inta niṉaiviṉai māṟṟa vēṇṭum. ōraḷavāvatu paṭittālaṉṟic camūkattil uyara muṭiyātu eṉṟa uṇmai avarkaḷ uḷḷattil uṇara vaḻivakai kāṇa vēṇṭum. uṭaṉ ceyalāṟṟiṉ takka payaṉ viḷaiyum.

oru kālattil varikaṭṭupavarukkuttāṉ caṭṭacapait tērtal vākkurimai eṉṟiruntatu. piṉ eḻutap paṭikkat, terintavarkaḷukku ellām vākkurimai uṇṭu eṉṟu viti vakukkappeṟṟatu. akkāla nikaḻccikaḷai nāṉ eṇṇip pārkkiṟēṉ. kirāmaṅkaḷilum nakaraṅkaḷilum eḻuttu vācaṉaiyē aṟiyāta palar. eppaṭiyāvatu paṭikka vēṇṭum-kai eḻuttāvatu pōṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu muyaṉṟu, ataṉ vaḻiyē vākkāḷar ākum takuti peṟa muyaṉṟaṉar. antac cila āṇṭukaḷil kaṇakku eṭuttiruntāl eḻuttaṟivu iyakka vaḷarcci naṉku viḷakkum. ittaṉaikkum antak kālattil aracāṅka utaviyō-aṭṭiṟ cālaikaḷō ilavaca nūl utaviyō piṟavō illai. palar yāraiyāvatu aṇṭik kaṟṟaṉar. anta nilai māṟi urimai peṟṟapiṉ ellārum innāṭṭu maṉṉarāka uyarntu viṭṭamaiyiṉ, paṭittavar paṭiyātavar -celvar vaṟiyar yāvarāyiṉum patiṉeṭṭu nirampap peṟṟāl vākkaḷarākavum, ataṉ vaḻic caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarākavum-ēṉ?-nāṭāḷum amaiccarākavum kūṭa ākalām eṉṟa nilai vantuviṭṭamaiyiṉ paṭippil kavaṉam kuṟaintatu. paṭikkāta mētai eṉṟa paṭṭamum vaḻaṅkap peṟum-eppaṭiyum campātikkalām eṉṟa nilaiyum kūṭavē cērntuviṭum piṉ mutiyōr kalvi eppaṭi vaḷarum? inta avalanilai jaṉanāyaka nāṭṭiṉai eppaṭit talaitūkka vaikkum? itaṉālēyē nāṭṭil palvēṟu pirivukaḷum cāti camayac caṇṭaikaḷum piṟakoṭumaikaḷum nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. oru kālattil kallātavar kaṟṟavar coṟkēṭṭu aṭaṅki nallavaṟṟai mēṟkoṇṭu vāḻntaṉar eṉak kāṇa muṭikiṟatu.

1977-78il ārampitta, nāṉ mēlē kāṭṭiya aracāṅkac cārpuṭaiya eḻuttaṟiviyakkam-mutiyōr kalvi muṟai pala vakaiyil iṭarppaṭṭu cilaviṭaṅkaḷil toṭaṅkiya paḷḷikaḷ mūṭappaṭṭu payaṉaṟṟa nilaiyil, 1989il putiya muyaṟciyiṉai aracāṅkam mēṟkoṇṭatu. ipputiya muṟai kēraḷam, kōvā, putuvai, kujarāt ākiya mānilaṅkḷil naṉku naṭaipeṟṟup payaṉum viḷaintuḷḷatu eṉpar. (p. 198). itaṉ vaḻi, pala taṉiyār niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ tāmē muṉ vantu, kallātār irukkumiṭam tāma ceṉṟu, avarkaḷ ōyvu peṟum nēram aṟintu, avarkaḷ maṉa nilaikkum maṉa niṟaivukkum ēṟṟa vakaiyil mella mella eḻuttaṟivittu ūkkuvikkum nilaiyiṉaik kāṇkiṉṟōm. itaṉālēyē aṇṭai mānilamāṉa kēraḷattilē nūṟṟukku nūṟu āṇum peṇṇum eḻuttaṟivu peṟṟuc ciṟakkiṟārkaḷ eṉa aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭu ippōtu tāṉ anta vaḻiyiṉaip piṉpaṟṟa niṉaikkiṟatu.

kēraḷam pōṉṟa oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil-kuṟainta aḷavil kallātavar vāḻum pakutikaḷil kāṇum veṟṟiyiṉai nāṭṭu veṟṟiyākak koḷḷa muṭiyātu. kallāmākkaḷ peruvāriyāka uḷḷa pakutikaḷil taṉi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum aracum cērntu, perutta muyaṟciyilum celavilum pāṭupaṭṭāl oru vēḷai veṟṟi kāṇalāmō, eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ. ēṟakkuṟaiya nūṟu āṇṭukaḷākak kēraḷamum-micōrāmum iṭaiyaṟātu pāṭupaṭṭu niṉṟa kāraṇattiṉālētāṉ aṅkellām niṟai nalamāka mutiyōr kalvi veṟṟi peṟa, kallātār illaiyeṉap pōṟṟap peṟuvatu aṟikiṟōm (p. 200). attakaiya muyaṟciyiṉai ellā mānilaṅkaḷum mēṟkoṇṭāl viraivil nāṭṭil kallātār illaiyāvar. ām! kaṟṟa āṇum peṇṇum akkallā maṉitaṉuṭaṉō peṇṇuṭaṉō paḻaki niṉṟu, avarkaḷ maṉanilai aṟintu mella mellak kalviyiṉ avaciyattaip pukaṭṭi ciṟu ciṟu vakaiyil eḻuttaṟivittu, avartam moḻi vaḷarkkum neṟiyil payiṟṟuvittu, tām cōrvu aṭaiyāmal taḷarcciyuṟāmal payiṟṟāḷarākac ceyalpurivarāyiṉ niccayamākap payaṉ viḷaiyum. iṅkē irāmamūrtti kuḻu inta mutiyōr kalvi vaḷarat tanta cila karuttukkaḷai appaṭiyē uṅkaḷmuṉ vaikkiṉṟēṉ avai aṉaittum, eṉṉālō-maṟṟavar aṉaivarālumō ēṟṟukkoḷḷak kūṭiyavai eṉṟu nāṉ koḷḷāviṭiṉum ikkaruttu ceyalāṟṟuvōrukkut tūṇṭukōlāka amaiyum eṉpatu eṉ tuṇivu.

RECOMMENDATIONS

Imperting of literacy should be placed in the context of the developmental needs of the adult. Adult education programmes should be accompanied by a wide range of measures relating to health, nutrition, housing, and employment needs. They should also address themselves in issues of fundamental rights, laws, secularism and democracy. After creating awareness in respect of these essentlal needs and issues the adult learner himself should be expected to ask for adult literacy, the stary should be in respect of creation of awareness for essential needs and from there work backward to adult literacy. ii) While the mass campaign strategy may be tried out further, the alternative model being employed by the Department of Education through Mahila Samakhya may be closely monitored and its application for adult literacy examined-considering that the objective of this project is to create awareness regarding issues of survival and there by generate felt needs. iii) The mass campaign strategy as well as the Mahila Samakhya model may be objectively evaluated to look for meanigful lessons for the future. iv) On most of the developmental problems and matters, realting to fundamental rights, social justice etc. the majority of the illiterates more often than not find themselves in positions of conflict with the official authorities. Therofore genuine initiatives for adult - education programmes. voluntary agencies, community groups, political parties and their mass organisations should be facilitated. v) While NLM goes ahead with its planned literacy campaign in the Eighth Five Year Plan period, an independent study group should be commissioned to evaluate the programme, particularly with a view to arrive at an understanding of what may be appropriate strategies to remove adult illiteracy in the quicker possible time . The evalution may also look into the various alternative models and study their relevance with respect to diverse sociocultural and political conditions in different parts of India. The mininum objective basis what approaches do not yield results, so that, five years later, at least those models may not be encouraged. vi) The Department of Education should coordinate with the Department of Rurel Development and Ministry of Labout and organise programmes for vocational skills for the adult illiterates—facilitating flow of funds from programmes like TRYSEM (Training of Youth for Self Employment). Community Polytechnics should also be involved in a large scale in imparting vocational skills amongst the adult illiterates. (This will enhance. the employability of the adult illiterates and thereby create awareness regarding basic needs and issues of life, in the process generating demand for adult literacy as a felt need ) vii) The non-literates should be placed in an environment in which they have constant interface with the challenge of the written word. viii) Illiterate adults are those who have either not had access to education or having had access, have been unable to complete their schooling. A person has to remain in school atleast for a minimum of four years to attain a relatively irreversible level of iiteracy. Literacy should be a form of basic training making it possible for the adult to acquire some knowledge as may be necessary. It is imperative that Universalisation of Elementary Education is given top priority in educational planning and resource allocation. The objective should be to ensure that no child in the early nineties shall grow into an illiterate adult in. the next century. (If this can be achieved, we would have then succeeded in controlling the chief contributor to illiteracy in India, i.e. low rate of participation in school education.) Pages-200 & 201 (Towards an Enlightened and Humane Society) ittuṭaṉ kaṟṟavar añcal vaḻiyē mēl nilaik kalvi payalum muṟaiyiṉaiyum eṇṇip pārkka vēṇṭum. kallātār orupuṟam ivvāṟu kaṟka, kaṟṟavar mēlum kaṟka virumpum nilai vaḷarntu varukiṟatu. intiya nāṭṭup palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ aṉaittum añcal vaḻik kalviyai aṟimukappaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa. itu paṟṟi muṉṉarē ōraḷavu kaṇṭōm. palkalaik kaḻakap paṭṭap paṭippiṉaiyum uyarpaṭṭap paṭippiṉaiyum añcal vaḻiyē kaṟṟut tarukiṉṟaṉa. ataṟkeṉa ovvoru palkalaik kaḻakamum taṉittuṟaiyiṉaiyē amaittup pāṭaṅkaḷait tayārittu aṉuppukiṉṟaṉa. oru cila muṟaiyāka iyaṅka, cila tavaṟukiṉṟaṉa. paṭṭam peṟa eṇṇip pala vakaiyil kaṉavu kāṇum palar allaluṟavum kāṇkiṟōm. pāṭaṅkaḷai muṟaiyāka aṉuppuvatum iṭaiyiṭaiyē kuṟitta iṭaṅkaḷil pala āciriyarkaḷaik koṇṭu pāṭaṅkaḷ naṭattuvatum muṟaiyāka naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. perumpālum paṇipuriyum āṭavarum makaḷirum ivaṟṟil cērntu payaṉpeṟa virumpukiṉṟaṉar; peṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum āyntu kaṇṭavarkaḷ ivarkaḷ taram muṟaiyākap payilupavar tarattiṉum tāḻntē uḷḷatu eṉpar. āṇṭoṉṟukku 80 nāḷkaḷ (nāḷaikku 5 maṇi nēram) nallāciriyarkaḷ kīḻē amarntu palvēṟu pāṭaṅkaḷai muṟaiyākap payiṉṟu, paruva iṭait tērvu, kālāṇṭu,araiyāṇṭut tērvukaḷ eḻuti tiruttap peṟṟu, taṅkaḷ tavaṟukaḷai avvappōtu uṇarntu tiruntum māṇavarkaḷōṭu ivarkaḷai oppiṭuvatu poruntātatutāṉ eṉiṉum ivarkaḷum muyaṉṟu, payiṉṟu veṟṟi peṟukiṉṟamai pārāṭṭutaṟkuriyatākum. āyiṉum orucila palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ palaraiyum tērcciyuṟac ceytāltāṉ nam palkalaik kaḻakattukku atika māṇavar varuvar-atika varuvāy kiṭaikkum eṉṟa eṇṇattil tavaṟukaḷ iḻaippatākak kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm. mēlum eṅkō oruvar pāṭaṅkaḷait tayārikka, vēṟu eṅkō oruvar āṇṭukku ōriru muṟai pāṭam naṭatta, eppōtō kaṭum kōṭaiyil orē muṟai tērvuk kaḷattil kutikkum ivarkaḷ nilai eṇṇattakka oṉṟākum. mēlum āyvukkaḷat toṭarpuṭaiya paṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum āciriyar payiṟcik kalvi pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum inta añcal vaḻik kalvikku appālē irukka vaittal nalamākum. intak koḷkaiyil palkalaik kaḻaka māṉiyak kuḻu viraivil cila cīrtiruttaṅkaḷaic ceyalpaṭutta niṉaikkum kuṟippukaḷ nāḷitaḻil veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa. taṟpōtu tiṟantaveḷip palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum ceyalāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. cila palkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷ ataṟkeṉat taṉittuṟaikaḷai amaittuḷḷaṉa. paṭippē aṟiyātavarō aṉṟic ciṟitu payiṉṟavarō nērē paṭṭat tērvukkuc cella vaḻi uṇṭu. cila palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ aṭippaṭaip paṭippai valiyuṟuttum oru nuḻaivut tērvil veṟṟi peṟutal oṉṟum pōtumāṉatu, itu avvaḷavu ciṟantatā? avaṟṟuḷ payiṉṟōr uṇmaiyil kaṟṟavarkaḷ āvārkaḷā eṉpatai varum kālamtāṉ namakku uṇarttum, eppaṭiyō nāṭṭil eḻuttaṟiyātavarkaḷai eḻuttaṟivu uḷḷavarkaḷākkutaṟkum caṟṟē paṭittavarkaḷ mēlum mēlum paṭittup paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟuvataṟkum palappala putiya muṟaikaḷ vakukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivai aṉaittum cemmaiyākac ceyalāṟṟiṉ niṉaitta payaṉaip peṟa iyalum, iṉṟēl kōṭik kaṇakkāṉa nāṭṭup paṇam celavāvatōṭu kālamum vīṇē kaḻintoḻintatāka muṭiyum. eṉavē ittakaiya kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷaik kaṭṭik kākkum poṟuppuḷḷavarkaḷ nāṭṭukkum camutāyattukkum tām āṟṟa vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamaiyāta-tēvaiyāṉa cēvaikaḷai maṉattil koṇṭu nāṭṭuk kalviyē mukkiyam eṉṟa uṇarvil ceyalāṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉak kēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ. ellārum kalvi kaṟṟu, mēlum kaṟṟu, mēṉmēlum kaṟṟu nāṭṭiṉ nalaṉaik kaṭṭik kākka vēṇṭum eṉpatē eṉ ācai!





8. toḻiṟ kalvi



* * *



toḻiṟkalvi urimai peṟṟapiṉ, caṟṟē vaḷarntirukkiṟatu eṉpatai ōraḷavu ēṟṟuk koḷḷattāṉ vēṇṭum. mattiya mānila aracukaḷ ittuṟaiyil karuttiruttic ceyalāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu collalām. tamiḻakac caṭṭamaṉṟattilē viraivil toḻiltuṟaiyil tamiḻnāṭu mutaliṭam peṟum eṉa mutalvar kūṟiyuḷḷatākac ceytit. tāḷvaḻi aṟiya muṭintatu. mattiya aracum taṉ nēraṭip pārvaiyilē nāṭṭiṉ tēvaikkēṟṟa vakaiyil niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷaip pala mānilaṅkaḷil amaittu varuvatu eṇṇat takkatu. eṉiṉum paranta pāratattukku inta vaḷarcci pōtātu eṉpatē aṟiñartam muṭivu. ataṟku ēṟpat toḻil evvaḷavu vaḷarcciyaṭaiyiṉum ataṉ vaḻiyē uruvākum poruḷkaḷiṉa vilai nāḷukku nāḷ uyarntu koṇṭētāṉ pōkiṟatu. itu vaḷarum nāṭṭukku. ēṟṟatākumā?

eṉ iḷamaik kālattilē (1925-28) āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu vakuppukaḷil nāṉ payiṉṟapōtu, ētāvatu oru toḻilaik kaṟkavēṇṭum eṉṟa muṟai iruntatu. nāṉ payiṉṟa vālājāpāt intumata pāṭacālaiyil payirt toḻil, pāy neytal, tuṇi necavu, nūl nūṟṟal, pottāṉ ceytal, taccavēlai, kayiṟu tirittuk kūṭai mutaliyaṉa ceytal pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷ kaṟṟuttarap peṟṟaṉ. māṇavaṉ ētēṉum oru toḻilait tavaṟātu kaṟṟuk koḷḷattāṉ vēṇṭum. appaṭiyē 1936il nāṉ paṇiyāṟṟat toṭaṅkiya kāñcipuram āntiracaṉ uyarnilaippaḷḷiyilum cila ciṟu kaittoḻilkaḷ pāṭat tiṭṭattil iṭam peṟṟiruntamai niṉaivukku varukiṟatu. pattām vakuppu aṉṟi, aṉṟaiya patiṉōrām vakuppu varaiyil payilum māṇavaṉ. ētēṉum oru toḻilaik kalviyuṭaṉ kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉpatu kaṭṭaḷai-muṟai. eṉiṉum kaṭṭāyam illaiyātalāl pala paḷḷikaḷ ivaṟṟai ēṟṟu naṭattavillai. nam nāṭṭil aṉṟum iṉṟum etuvum kaṭṭāyamākkiṉāltāṉ naṭakkumpōlum. eṉiṉum mēlē 11ām vakuppukaḷil taṭṭeḻuttu pōṉṟavai toḻil amaippil iṭampeṟa, paḷḷi iṟutit tērvil avaiyum pāṭamāka ēṟkappeṟṟup pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē antiyar āṭci irunta anta nāḷilēyē paḷḷiyil payilum māṇavar ēṭṭuk kalviyuṭaṉ nāṭṭukkup payaṉpaṭum toḻiṟkalvi oṉṟiṉaik muṟaiyākap payila vaḻivakai iruntatu. āṉāl urimai peṟṟapiṉ anta nilai illai eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ. perumpālum 6 mutal 11vatu varaiyil payilupavar ittakaiya toḻil oṉṟiṉaik kaṟṟukkoṇṭāl, avar paḷḷippaṭippu muṭintatum vēlai tēṭi alaiyāml vīṭṭilēyē ētēṉum toḻil ceytu vāḻa vaḻi iruntatu inta marapu ēṉō urimai peṟṟa pāratattil otukkap peṟṟatu? -

irājāji mutalvarāka irunta kālattil toḻil kalviyiṉaic cāti aṭippaṭaiyil cārtti avaṉavaṉ taṉtaṉ cātikkamaitta toḻilaik kaṟkavēṇṭum eṉṟu viti amaittār eṉṟum itaṉālēyē atu pala periya etirppukkaḷiṭaiyē eṭupaṭāmal viṭupaṭṭup pōyiṟṟu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. paḷḷikaḷil pala toḻilkaḷai amaittu, vēṇṭiyavar vēṇṭiya toḻilaip payilalām eṉa vitittu, avaṟṟiṟkut tērvum naṭattic cāṉṟitaḻ tara ēṟpāṭu ceytiruppārāyiṉ attoḻilmuṟai ōraḷavu nāṭṭil vaḷarntirukkum. ‘varuṇācirama’ marapu eṉa atu otukkappaṭṭatu eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ.

ka.-8 1974il putiya kalvi muṟai (10+2+3) pukuttiyapōtu mēnilai vakuppukaḷil (11 & 12) toḻil pirivu eṉṟu (Vocational Course) oṉṟu amaikkappeṟṟatu. aṉṟu anta mēnilaippaḷḷik kuḻuvil nāṉ uṟuppiṉarāka iruntapōtu, itai valiyuṟuttip pala toḻilkaḷai ēṟpaṭutti, oṉṟiṉai māṇavar paṟṟip paṭara vaḻivakukkumāṟu kūṟiṉēṉ. eṉiṉum atu kāla kaṭṭattil veṟum taṭṭaccum curukkeḻuttum eṉṟa vakaiyil amaintatu. atilum pala māṇavar paṅku koṇṭaṉar. 1987-88 mēnilai vakuppil payiṉṟa cumār 3.5 ilaṭcam māṇavaril 96,000 pēr (24%) intat toḻiṟkalvi payiṉṟār eṉa aracāṅkak kaṇakku tarappeṟukiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum itil payilpavarkaḷ kallūriyil cēra vāyppu illāta nilai uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kallūriyil cērkkum māṇavaril nūṟṟukkup pattu māṇavaraiyē cērkka viti amaittaṉar pōlum. pala kallūrikaḷ ataiyum piṉpaṟṟuvatillai. eṉavē ataṉ vaḷarcciyum taṭaippaṭṭatu eṉalām. nāṉ muṉṉarē kāṭṭiyapaṭi ivarkaḷai aracāṅka eḻuttar patavikku mutaṉilai tantu eṭuttuk koḷḷal poruntuvatākum. ippaṭiyē immēnilai vakuppukaḷil āciriyar payiṟci vakuppum iruntatu. atil payiṉṟavarkaḷai-mēlum attuṟaiyilēyē ōrāṇṭu īrāṇṭu payiṟci peṟacceytu, paḷḷikaḷil āciriyarkaḷāka niyamikka vaḻi ceytirukkalām. avvāṟillāmal avarkaḷum nāṟcantiyil niṉṟu naliya vēṇṭi vantamaiyiṉ antat tuṟaiyum aṇmaiyil mūṭappaṭṭatu eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ. ivvāṟu ākka neṟikkeṉa vakukkappeṟṟa pātaikaḷ aracāṅka ūkkutal illāta kāraṇattiṉālēyē aḻikkappaṭum ōr avalanilai nāṭṭil, kāṇa varunta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. toḻiṟkalvi eṉṟāl ētō poṟiyiyal, maruttuvam ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil amainta paḷḷikaḷ, kallūrikaḷil payilutal eṉṟē niṉaittirukkiṟōm. cātāraṇa kuṭicait toḻilkaḷukkum payiṟciyum pātukāppum tēvai eṉpatai maṟantu viṭukiṟōm. nāṉ muṉpu kāṭṭiya mutiyōr kalvi neṟiyil ittakaiya toḻiṟkalviyum iṭampeṟiṉ, itaik kaṟṟuk koḷḷuvatōṭu varupavarkaḷ ēṭṭuk kalviyilum payiṟci peṟa vāyppu uṇṭu. paḻaṅkālattil iruntatu pōṉṟu paḷḷiyilēyē-ciṟappākak kirāmappuṟa paḷḷikaḷilēyē kait toḻilkaḷ kaṟpikkappeṟal vēṇṭum, peruñcelvarkaḷaip perum kaittoḻil peruka ūkki ‘uḷḷavaṉukkuk koṭukkappaṭum; illātavaṉiṭattiliruntu uḷḷatum eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭum’ eṉṟapaṭi, nāṭṭu naṭappum ceyal muṟaiyum piṟavum iruntāl eppaṭit toḻil vaḷarum: eppaṭi ‘ellārum ellāc celvamum peṟṟu’ vāḻa muṭiyum? āḷupavar ivaṟṟaiyellām cintittu uṭaṉ takka vakaiyil ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭumeṉak kēṭṭukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ. nāṭu vāḻa atuvē ciṟantatu. iṉṟu ciṟutoḻil vaḷarccikku ūkkamum ākkamum tara aracu muṉvarukiṟatu. ataṟkeṉa amaiccum taṉittuṟaiyum kūṭa uḷḷaṉa eṉa aṟikiṟēṉ. āṉāl ataṟku māṉiyamākat tarappeṟum aracāṅkap paṇam ententa vakaiyilō-vaḻaṅkupavarukkuk kaiyūṭṭu, piṟavakaic celavukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṟkup payaṉpaṭukiṟatu eṉpar.

nāṭṭil toḻiṟkalvi ōṅki varukiṟateṉiṉum pōtiya aḷavil vaḷarcci aṭaiyavillai. intiyak kalvikkuḻu itupaṟṟit taṉ karuttiṉait terivittu (National Policy of Education) ataṉ vaḷarccikku vaḻikāṇa muyalkiṉṟatu. tiru. irāmamūrtti kuḻuvum ataṉ karuttukkaḷai ārāyntu vaḷarccikku vaḻikāṭṭiyuḷḷatu.

tamiḻnāṭu uṭpaṭa nāṉku mānilaṅkaḷil urimam peṟṟum peṟāmalum pala poṟiyiyaṟ kallūrikaḷum toḻiṟ paḷḷikaḷum iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum avaṟṟuḷ pala tarattil tāḻntuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum akkuṟippu kāṭṭukiṟatu. ām! tamiḻnāṭṭil aracāṅkam ceṉṟa āṇṭu oru kallūriyōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, vēṟupāṭuṟṟa nilaiyiṉai nāṭaṟiyum. mēlum āḷupavarukku uṟṟavarākavum palkalaik kaḻakattukkup palavakaiyil vēṇṭiyavarākavum iruppiṉ poṟiyiyaṟ kallūri maṭṭumaṉṟi, putitāka entak kallūriyum toṭaṅka vāyppu uḷḷatai nāṭṭu naṭavaṭikkaikaḷ naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟaṉavē. māṟimāṟi varum āḷupavar kaippāvaikaḷākap pala kallūrikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa-avai ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭiya nilaiyil māṇavarkaḷait tiṟaṉaṟintavarkaḷāka ākkum vakaiyil irukkiṉṟaṉavā eṉpatu aiyattukkiṭamāka uḷḷatē. maṟṟum peṇkaḷukkuc camaurimai eṉṟu mēṭaiyilum caṭṭamaṉṟattilum muḻaṅkum nilaiyilum toḻiṟ. kallūriyil 12% cāṉṟitaḻ paḷḷikaḷil 17% tāṉ payilkiṉṟaṉar. cīrmarapiṉar aṉṟi otukkappaṭṭavar paṭṭap paṭippil 5% cāṉṟitaḻ paḷḷiyil 9% uḷḷaṉar (pa 237). inta avala nilaiyil makaḷir muṉṉēṟṟamum tāḻntōr uyarvatum muyaṟkompu-ākāyappūp pōṉṟatākum.

ittakaiya toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷ urimam peṟṟavai peṟātavai naṉku iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉavā eṉakkāṇal araciṉ kaṭamaiyākum. nalla āyvuk kūṭaṅkaḷ, nallāciriyarkaḷ, nalla kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ illāmalēyē pala iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉap pēcukiṉṟaṉar-aṟikkai viṭukiṉṟaṉar. aracō palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷō inta aval nilaikaḷaip paṟṟi eṇṇuvatillai pōlum. vēṇṭiyavarkaḷāyiṉ oṉṟum illāmalē ellā vakuppukaḷaiyum tarutalum vēṇṭātavarāyiṉ ellām iruntum ētō kāraṇam kāṭṭi oṉṟum tarātu oḻippatum palkalaik kaḻakac ceyalāka amaivatu eṉkiṉṟaṉar.

poṟiyiyal, maruttuvattuṟai pōṉṟavaṟṟilum kālnaṭai maruttuvam, vēḷāṇmai pōṉṟavaṟṟilum takka aṉupavam mikkavarkaḷaiyē āciriyarkaḷāka niyamikka vēṇṭum. iṅkēyum patavi uyarvu mutaliyavaṟṟilēyum iṉṉār iṉiyār eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭappeṟukiṟatu. eṉpar. aṭippaṭait tēvaiyāṉa cātaṉaṅkaḷ-karuvikaḷ kūṭa illāmal eppaṭi ivai iyaṅka muṭiyum mattiya aracu ivaṟṟukkeṉa vaḻaṅkum perum tokaikaḷai mānila aracu taṉ celavukaḷukkeṉa vēṟu vakaiyil celavu ceytuviṭalām. itaṉāl muṟaiyākac ceyalpaṭum paḷḷikaḷ kallūrikaḷai antarattil viṭa nēriṭukiṉṟatu. 1989-90 il muṭikkap peṟṟa paṇikaḷukkum ceymuṟaikaḷukkum cila paḷḷikaḷukku 1990 iṭaiyilēyē mattiya aracu muḻu māṉiyat tokaiyiṉai mānila, aracukku aṉuppiyum, iṉṉum attokaikaḷ uriya kalvi nilaikaḷukkuc ceṉṟu cēravillai eṉkiṉṟaṉar. piṉ avai evvāṟu naṉku ceyalpaṭum? vēliyē payirai, mēyum vakaiyil ivvāṟu pala māṉiyaṅkaḷ iṭaiyil aracāṅkattālē avalamākkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟaiya aracu itaikkaṇṭu āvaṉa ceyyum eṉa nampukiṟēṉ.

kirāma makkaḷai īṭupaṭuttum vakaiyil palkalait toḻiṟ paḷḷikaḷ (Polytecnics) iyaṅkavēṇṭum. pallāyirakkaṇakkil paṇam koṭuttāltāṉ iṭam kiṭaikkum eṉṟa nilai māṟavēṇṭum. kaṇṇiruntum kuruṭarāy-kātiruntum ceviṭarāy aṉaittaiyum kaṇṭu vāḷāliruntāl, nāṭu iṉṉum iḻinilaiyaittāṉ aṭaiyum. ivaṟṟai eṇṇi nāṭāḷum nallavarum patavi vakikkum paṭṭatārikaḷum aluvalarkaḷum piṟarum nāṭṭu nilaṉilē akkaṟai koṇṭu ceyalpaṭutal naṉṟākum. mukkiyamākat toḻiṟkalvi vaḷara aṉaivarum oṉṟic ceyalāṟṟiṉāltāṉ payaṉ uṇṭu. camutāyattōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya toḻilkaḷai ūrtōṟum amaittu; avaṟṟiṟkut toḻiliyal cāṉṟitaḻ allatu paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷait takkavāṟu amaittu, vēṇṭiya upkaraṇaṅkaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum tantu aracu ūkka vēṇṭum. kirāmaṅkaḷil payiriṭuvōr pāti nērattai vīṇākkāmal nāṭṭukkut tēvaiyāṉa poruḷkaḷai uṟpatti ceytutarum nilai vaḷara vēṇṭum. paṭittu viṭṭu vēlai illātirukkum nilaiyum itaṉāl illaiyākum ellārum nakariltāṉ uttiyōkam pārkka vēṇṭum eṉṟa nilai iruntāl pala kōṭiyāka vāḻum nam nāṭṭuk kirāma makkaḷ kati eṉṉāvatu? toḻil mayam kirāmaṅkaḷil toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.

toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷait toṭaṅkuvataṟkup palkalaik kaḻakam, mānila aracu, mattiya aracu ākiya aṉaittiliruntum icaivu peṟutal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. vēṇṭuvōrtam tēvaiyiṉai aṟintuvarum viṇṇappaṅkaḷai uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ kavaṉittu, toṭaṅkuvataṟku mūṉṟu mātaṅkaḷukku muṉṉarē muṭivu eṭuttu mattiya, mānila aracukaḷum palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum āṇai aṉuppiṉāl naṉ māṇavarkaḷ cērkkap peṟṟup payaṉaṭaivar. eṉavē putiya toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷ tiṟakkavum putiya pāṭaṅkaḷ toṭaṅkavum varum viṇṇappaṅkaḷai uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ ārāyntu iru aracukaḷum palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum ceyalāṟṟa vēṇṭiya kaṭamaiyiṉai maṟattalākātu. icaivu vaḻaṅkuvatilum nēril tiṟaṉaṟi kuḻukkaḷ amaittu nēril ceṉṟu kaṇṭu amaippāḷarkaḷ takka vakaiyil tēvaiyāṉavaṟṟaic ceytirukkiṟārkaḷā eṉak kaṇṭē icaivu vaḻaṅkavēṇṭum. viraintu ceyalpaṭiṉ viṭivu uṇṭu viḷaivum uṇṭu.

toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷaip pala toḻiṟ kūṭaṅkaḷōṭu iṇaikkum vakaiyil amaikka vēṇṭum. veṟum ēṭṭup paṭippum aṅkē ceyyum cila kaḷa āyvukaḷum pōtā! ‘ēṭṭuk kalvi kavaikku utavātu’ eṉpatu ellāk kalvikkum poruntum eṉṟālum toḻiṟ kalvikku mika mikamukkiyamāṉatu. ‘ēṭṭuc curakkāy kaṟikku utavātu’ eṉṟa maṟṟoru paḻamoḻiyum itaṟkup poruntum. maruttuvakkallūrikku uṭaṉ iṇaintē ovvoru maruttuvamaṉai irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa viti iruppatu pōṉṟu poṟiyiyal pōṉṟa toḻiṟ kallūrikaḷukkum ōr uyariya toḻiṟcālai allatu toḻiṟpēṭṭai irukka vēṇṭum eṉa viti ceyya vēṇṭum. payilum māṇavar pāti nērattilō-aṉṟit tēvaiyāṉa nērattilō attoḻiṟcālaiyil nēriya payiṟci peṟal vēṇṭum, nāṉ muṉṉē kirāmaṅkaḷukkuk kāṭṭiya ciṟutoḻil neṟippaṭi, intak kallūrikaḷil paṭikkum māṇavar peruntoḻilō ciṟu toḻilō tēvaiyāṉāl kirāmantōṟum tāmē toṭaṅka untutal peṟṟavarātal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl palakōṭi toḻiṟkalvikkuc celaviṭutalāl payaṉillai, aracāṅkamum toḻiṟ kallūri amaip pōrum inta vakaiyil karuttirutti āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭumeṉakkēṭṭuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ.

toḻiṟ kalviyiṉ ovvoru tuṟaiyiṉaiyum taṉit taṉi ārāyiṉ atuvē perunūlākum. ataṟku ēṟṟa taṉit takutiyum eṉakku illai eṉpatai nāṉ naṉku aṟivēṉ. maruttuvam, poṟiyiyal, kaṇippoṟi, ciṟu toḻilkaḷ aṉaittilum avvattuṟaiyil vallavarkaḷait taṉittaṉiyāka mānila maiya aracukaḷ aḻaittu, makkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟa pakutikku ēṟṟa takka payaṉ viḷaiyattakka vakaiyil ceyalpāṭukaḷum amaiyum vakaiyil pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum payiṟṟu muṟaiyiṉaiyum kaḷappaṇikaḷaiyum amaittu, ataiyum cari pārkka ōr uyarnilaik kuḻuvaiyum ovvoru tuṟaikkum amaittu āvaṉa kāṇiṉ nāṭu nalampeṟum. eppōtō orumuṟai eṅkō talainakaril kuḷukuḷu aṟaiyil uṭkārntu koṇṭu tiṭṭam tīṭṭiṉāl payaṉillai. iṭantōṟum, ceṉṟu-ūrtōṟum nilavum nilai kaṇṭu avvannilaikku ēṟṟavakaiyil paḷḷi, kallūrikaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, toḻiṟ kūṭaṅkaḷai amaittu avaṟṟaip pōṟṟip pātutākka vēṇṭum. nāṉ mēlēkāṭṭiyapaṭi, avvattuṟaikaḷukku ēṟṟa toḻiṟ kūṭaṅkaḷ iṇaikkappeṟal vēṇṭum; aṉṟi amaikkappeṟa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟaṉṟi veṟum toḻiṟ paḷḷi, kallūrikaḷai maṭṭum toṭaṅki viṭṭāl maṟṟoru vēlai illāp paṭṭāḷamē uruvākum. mēlum nāṭṭil pala cikkalkaḷum koṭumaikaḷum piṟa iḻinilaikaḷum tōṉṟa vaḻivakukkum. nāṭāḷum nallavarkaḷ ivaṟṟaiyellām eṇṇic ceyalāṟṟuvārkaḷāyiṉ nāṭu nalam peṟum. nām ulakattil uyarvōm. uṇmai vāḻvum nalamum malarum.

intat toḻiṟkalvi paṟṟit tiru. irāmamūrtti kuḻuviṉar āyntu teḷinta muṭivukaḷai neṟippaṭutta vēṇṭip parinturai ceytuḷḷaṉar (pak. 243-248). ovvoru vakaiyiṉaiyum naṉku ārāyntu ataṟku eṉṉeṉṉa ceyyavēṇṭum eṉa vakaippaṭuttik kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ittuṟaiyil paṇipurivōr aṉaivarumē ivaṟṟai neṟi aṟintu pōṟṟal vēṇṭum. intap parinturaikaḷē muṭivu eṉṟu koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉa nāṉ kūṟamāṭṭēṉ. ivai muṉṉēṟṟap paṭikaḷuḷ cila. teḷiyat teḷiya, ārāya ārāya ulaka vaḷarccikku oppa nam nāṭṭut toḻiṟkalviyum ataṟkeṉa amainta piṟa tuṇainilaik karuvikaḷum iṭamum kālamum piṟavum amaiyap pārttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. cila nāṭukaḷ muyalvatu pōṉṟu aṟiviyal neṟi aḻivuppātaikku vaḻi kōlāmal ākkap pātaikku vaḻi kōluvatāy-ulakac camutāya vāḻvukku ukantatāy amaiya vaḻikāṇa vēṇṭum. yāṇṭum iṉpam niṟaiya-ellārum ellāc celvamum peṟa-vaiyakam vaḷamuṟa-kalvit tuṟaiyiṉ ellāppakutikaḷum muyaṉṟu orumai uṇarvil ceyalāṟṟiṉ nāṭum ulakamum, uyyum uyarum annāḷ viraintu varuvatāka!

nalla āciriyariṉ ilakkaṇam

kulaṉ aruḷ teyvaṅ koḷkai mēṉmai

kalaipayil teḷivu kaṭṭurai vaṉmai

nilam, malai niṟaikōl, malarnikar māṭciyum

ulakiyal aṟivōṭu uyar kuṇam iṇaiyavum

amaipavaṉ nūl urai āciriyaṉṉē.



(naṉṉūl)





pāṭam collum muṟai

ītal iyalpē iyampum kālai

kālamum iṭaṉum vālitiṉ nōkkic

ciṟantuḻi iruntutaṉ teyvam vāḻtti

uraikkap paṭuporuḷ uḷḷat tamaittu

viraiyāṉ vekuḷāṉ virumpi mukamalarntu

koḷvōṉ koḷvakai aṟintu avaṉ uḷaṅkoḷak

kōṭṭamil maṉattiṉ nūlkoṭuttal eṉpa.



(naṉṉūl)





pāṭamkēṭkum muṟai

kōṭalmarapē kūṟum kālaip

poḻutoṭu ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭal muṉiyāṉ

kuṇattoṭu paḻaki avaṉkuṟippiṉ cārntu

irueṉa iruntu coleṉac collip

parukuvaṉ aṉṉa ārvattiṉ ākic

cittirap pāvaiyiṉ attakavu aṭaṅkic

cevi vāyāka neñcu kaḷaṉākak

kēṭṭavai kēṭṭavai viṭātu uḷattu amaittup

pō eṉap pōtal eṉmaṉār pulavar.



(naṉṉūl)





inta miṉṉūlaip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇaiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭaittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇaiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷai varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇaintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷai aṉaivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟaiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻai ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻaikaḷai nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭaippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uraiyai nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:
